Abstract: We present StyleText, a large-scale dataset and benchmark for localized scene-text inpainting with style preservation. StyleText contains 28,518 image-mask-prompt triplets grouped into 9,932 scene families, enabling controlled evaluation of text legibility and visual consistency under shared scene context. We construct the dataset with an automated pipeline that combines LLM prompt templating, Flux-based source generation with key-value (KV) cache injection, OCR-based semantic filtering, polygon mask extraction, and mask-conditioned FluxFill augmentation. We define a reproducible evaluation protocol using normalized OCR metrics (word accuracy and character error rate) and CLIP image-image similarity with explicit preprocessing. A FluxFill+LoRA baseline trained on StyleText improves OCR accuracy substantially over initialization while maintaining scene style consistency, establishing a strong reference point for future comparisons.

</details>


### [109] [Attention Hijacking: Response Manipulation Across Queries in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17310)
*Zhiqiang Wang,Dongrui Liu,Yan Li,Zonghao Ying,Wei Xue,Wenhan Luo,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Attention Hijacking攻击方法，通过操纵注意力分布中图像token的主导地位，实现跨不同文本查询的视觉语言模型响应操控，显著提升了对抗样本的跨查询可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对视觉语言模型的对抗攻击在固定扰动与不同文本查询配对时效果显著下降，缺乏能够跨多样化用户查询保持有效的攻击方法。

Method: 提出Attention Hijacking攻击，通过增强视觉token对目标响应token的注意力权重、同时抑制文本token的竞争性影响，显式地引导内部注意力分布朝向持久的图像主导模式。

Result: 在多种主流VLM上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了跨不同目标响应和未见查询的跨查询可迁移性，并能有效扩展到多种攻击场景。

Conclusion: 注意力稳定性在视觉语言模型的可迁移响应操控中扮演关键角色，本研究为理解VLM的鲁棒性提供了新视角。

Abstract: Existing adversarial attacks on vision-language models (VLMs) can steer model outputs toward attacker-specified target responses, but their effectiveness often degrades when the same perturbed input is paired with different textual queries. This paper studies cross-query response manipulation, where a single adversarial example is expected to remain effective across diverse user queries. We first analyze the limitations of existing attacks and find that successful transfer is closely associated with preserving an image-dominant attention pattern during response generation. Motivated by the observation, we propose \textbf{Attention Hijacking}, a novel adversarial attack that explicitly steers internal attention distributions toward a persistent image-dominant pattern. By amplifying the influence of visual tokens on target response tokens while suppressing the competing influence of textual tokens, our method reduces the dependence of the manipulated output on the specific wording of the query. Extensive experiments on widely used VLMs show that Attention Hijacking substantially improves cross-query transferability across diverse target responses and unseen queries. The method also extends effectively to multiple attack scenarios, offering new insights into the role of attention stability in transferable response manipulation for VLMs.

</details>


### [110] [SpecSem-Net: Integrating Spectral and Semantic Features for Robust AI-generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17311)
*Zixi Wei,Huixuaun Zhang,Xiaojun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecSem-Net，首个引入语义引导频谱去噪机制的高保真AI生成视频检测框架，通过傅里叶变换提取高频特征并利用门控融合机制抑制频谱噪声，在商业生成器基准和公开数据集上分别达到87.25%和95.59%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器过度依赖语义特征，忽略了细微的频谱伪影，导致对高保真AI生成视频检测效果不佳，亟需更鲁棒的检测方法以防止合成内容被用于虚假信息传播。

Method: 设计基于傅里叶变换滤波的频谱模块提取高频特征，并提出门控融合机制自适应融合语义上下文以抑制频谱噪声，实现语义引导的频谱去噪。

Result: 在包含5个最先进商业生成器的基准上达到87.25%准确率，在公开数据集上达到95.59%准确率，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 语义引导的频谱去噪机制能有效提升高保真AI生成视频检测性能，为应对最新顶级生成模型的检测挑战提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The remarkable visual fidelity of recent commercial video generative models, such as Sora and Veo, renders robust AI-generated video detection increasingly essential to prevent synthetic content from being indistinguishable from real videos and exploited for disinformation. However, existing detectors often fail due to an over-reliance on increasingly realistic semantic features, neglecting subtle spectral artifacts. In this paper, we propose SpecSem-Net, the first framework to introduce a semantic-guided spectral denoising mechanism specifically for high-fidelity AI-generated video detection. Specifically, we design a spectral module to extract high-frequency features via Fourier-Transform based filtering. Furthermore, to reduce misjudgments arising from spectral noise, we employ a Gated Merging Mechanism to adaptively fuse semantic context, effectively mitigating spectral noise. Additionally, to evaluate detector performance on the latest top-tier generative models, we construct a comprehensive benchmark comprising 5 SOTA commercial generators. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SpecSem-Net outperforms existing methods, achieving accuracies of 87.25% and 95.59% on our benchmark and public datasets, respectively.

</details>


### [111] [VISTA: Triplet-Supervised Video Style Transfer with Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17312)
*Yiren Song,Wangzi Yao,Haofan Wang,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISTA-1000合成数据集和基于扩散变换器的上下文视频风格迁移框架，通过联合建模与解耦风格、内容和运动，解决了视频风格迁移中的时间不一致性问题，在风格保真度、时间一致性和内容保留方面达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频风格迁移方法主要依赖启发式时间传播来强制执行一致性，在遮挡、去遮挡和长程运动下容易出现漂移和闪烁伪影。根本瓶颈在于缺乏大规模三元组数据和能够联合建模并解耦风格、内容与运动的 principled 训练范式。

Method: 1) 构建VISTA-1000合成数据集，包含1,000种风格及其对应的风格参考、干净视频和风格化视频的运动对齐三元组；2) 提出基于扩散变换器（Diffusion-Transformer）的上下文视频风格迁移框架，配备轻量级风格适配器以实现鲁棒风格提取。

Result: 在风格保真度、时间一致性和内容保留方面达到最先进的性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 通过大规模合成数据与扩散变换器架构的结合，有效解决了视频风格迁移中的时间不一致问题，验证了联合建模风格-内容-运动解耦范式的有效性。

Abstract: Video style transfer aims to render videos in a target artistic style while preserving content, structure, and motion. While image stylization has advanced rapidly, video stylization remains challenging due to temporal inconsistency. Most existing methods stylize frames or keyframes and enforce consistency via heuristic temporal propagation, which is brittle under occlusions, disocclusions, and long-term motion, leading to drift and flickering artifacts. We argue that a fundamental bottleneck lies in the lack of large-scale triplet data and a principled training paradigm that jointly models and disentangles style, content, and motion.To address this, we introduce VISTA-1000, a synthetic dataset with 1,000 styles and motion-aligned triplets of style reference, clean video, and stylized video, and propose a diffusion-transformer-based in-context video style transfer framework with a lightweight style adapter for robust style extraction. Extensive experiments demonstrate SOTA performance in style fidelity, temporal consistency, and content preservation.

</details>


### [112] [Single-Sample Black-Box Membership Inference Attack against Vision-Language Models via Cross-modal Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17341)
*Jiaqing Li,Yajuan Lu,Xiaochuan Shi,Gang Wu,ZhongYuan Wang,Chao Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视觉-语言模型（VLM）的成员推理攻击（MIA）方法，利用跨模态语义对齐差异，在严格黑盒和单样本场景下通过量化图像-文本嵌入空间的对齐程度来判断数据是否属于训练集，无需内部logits或大规模统计分布，在多个开源和闭源VLM上取得显著优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM的成员推理攻击方法存在关键瓶颈：灰盒方法依赖内部logits（实际API中通常受限），黑盒方法依赖大规模统计分布（单样本场景下难以应用）。亟需一种在严格黑盒、单样本设定下可行的MIA方法。

Method: 提出基于跨模态语义对齐的MIA框架。观察到：成员图像由于训练记忆作用，与原始文本描述的对齐程度显著更强；而非成员图像的生成描述可能偏离原始视觉内容。在联合嵌入空间中量化这种对齐差异，绕过对内部logits或大规模分布的不现实假设，实现严格黑盒、单样本下的攻击。

Result: 在三个开源和两个闭源VLM上进行广泛实验。在VL-MIA/Flickr数据集上，对LLaVA-1.5的AUC达0.821，显著优于现有基线方法。在多种图像扰动下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在严格黑盒、单样本设定下有效实现了VLM的成员推理攻击，且具有实用性和鲁棒性，为评估VLM数据安全风险提供了新途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved remarkable success, yet their reliance on massive datasets and unintended memorization of training data raise significant data security risk. Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) aim to assess these risks by determining whether a data sample was included in a model's training set. However, existing MIA methods against VLMs face critical bottlenecks: gray-box method relies on internal logits that are typically restricted in real-world Application Programming Interfaces (APIs), while black-box method depends on large-scale statistical distributions, which struggle in single-sample scenarios. To this end, we investigate MIAs from the perspective of cross-modal semantic alignment, and observe that member images exhibit significantly stronger image-caption alignment due to training memorization, whereas generated captions for non-members may deviate from the original visual content. Leveraging this insight, we propose a novel MIA framework designed for strict black-box and single-sample setting that quantifies such alignment within a joint embedding space, thereby bypassing these unrealistic assumptions. We conducted extensive experiments on three open-source and two closed-source VLMs. On the VL-MIA/Flicker dataset, our method achieves an AUC of 0.821 against LLaVA-1.5, significantly outperforming existing baselines. Furthermore, it remains robust under diverse image perturbations, highlighting its practicality.

</details>


### [113] [GraphMAR: Geometry-Aware Graph Learning Framework for Spatially Adaptive CT Metal Artifact Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17343)
*Zilong Li,Chenglong Ma,Yiming Lei,Yuanlin Li,Jing Han,Jiannan Liu,Huidong Xie,Junping Zhang,Yi Zhang,Hongming Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphMAR，一种基于图建模的几何感知图像域CT金属伪影去除框架。通过构建几何密度图定位伪影区域，并设计GraphMoE模块实现空间自适应伪影去除，在模拟和真实数据集上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT金属伪影去除方法中，正弦域方法依赖原始投影数据、实用性受限；图像域方法灵活但缺乏几何引导，导致伪影定位能力不足、效果欠佳。本文旨在为图像域MAR引入显式的几何引导，实现伪影的显式定位与空间自适应去除。

Method: 提出GraphMAR框架：1) 从金属掩模构建几何图，导出几何密度图以粗定位伪影区域；2) 设计GraphMoE模块，在特征空间构建极坐标伪影图，通过图路由机制将不同专家分配到不同空间区域；3) 将学习到的路由图与几何密度图对齐，实现显式伪影定位和区域自适应伪影去除。

Result: 在模拟和真实CT数据集上的实验表明，GraphMAR在伪影去除性能上优于现有方法，同时提供了显式且可解释的伪影定位能力。

Conclusion: 本文首次将图建模引入CT金属伪影去除任务，并首次在图像域实现了显式的伪影识别，兼顾了恢复质量和可解释性。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) metal artifact reduction (MAR) aims to reduce the severe streaking artifacts induced by metallic implants and other high-density objects. Effective MAR generally requires both accurate artifact localization and artifact removal. Sinogram-domain methods can exploit explicit geometric cues, such as metal traces, to identify metal-corrupted measurements, while requiring raw projection data, which is often unavailable in clinical and practical scenarios. Image-domain methods are more flexible and widely applicable, yet they usually lack comparable geometric guidance, limiting their ability to localize artifacts and leading to suboptimal results. To address this limitation, we propose GraphMAR, a geometry-aware learning framework for explicit artifact identification and spatially adaptive MAR in the image domain. The key idea is to introduce graph-based geometric modeling as an image-domain analogue of sinogram metal traces. Specifically, we first construct a geometric graph from the metal mask and derive a geometric density graph that coarsely localizes artifact-prone regions according to inter-implant geometry. We then design GraphMoE, a graph-routed mixture-of-experts module that builds a polar-coordinate artifact graph in feature space and adaptively routes different experts to different spatial regions for MAR. By aligning the learned routing maps with the geometric density graph, GraphMAR provides explicit and interpretable artifact localization while enabling region-adaptive artifact reduction. Experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate that GraphMAR achieves superior MAR performance compared with existing methods. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to introduce graph-based modeling for CT MAR and to enable explicit artifact identification in the image domain, improving both restoration quality and interpretability.

</details>


### [114] [GeoHand: Unlocking Prior Geometry Knowledge for Monocular 3D Hand Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17354)
*Weiquan Lin,Yaoqing Hu,Liangchen Dai,Xu Tang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoHand提出利用冻结的MoGe2基础模型提取几何先验，通过GeoAdapter适配到手部重建、门控跨模态融合策略平衡几何与外观特征、以及关键点查询迭代精化器（KQIR）进行局部关节校正，在FreiHAND、DexYCB和HO3Dv3上达到SOTA，尤其在严重遮挡和手物交互场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目RGB外观特征因自遮挡和手物交互存在严重的深度歧义性，而原始传感器捕获的深度图噪声大、不完整，难以用于精细的手部重建。

Method: (1) 利用冻结的基础单目几何估计器MoGe2提取高质量几何先验；(2) 引入面向手部重建的GeoAdapter对几何特征进行空间重校准；(3) 采用门控跨模态token融合策略自适应整合几何与外观特征；(4) 设计关键点查询迭代精化器（KQIR），利用投影关节点查询几何感知图像特征进行空间校正。

Result: 在FreiHAND、DexYCB和HO3Dv3数据集上达到最先进性能，尤其在严重遮挡和手物交互场景下表现突出。

Conclusion: 通过全局几何解歧与局部精化相结合的统一管线，GeoHand有效解决了单目手部重建中的深度歧义性问题。

Abstract: Monocular 3D hand reconstruction is intrinsically a geometric problem, yet RGB appearance features alone often struggle to resolve severe ambiguities caused by self-occlusions and hand-object interactions. While introducing depth can explicitly provide spatial cues, raw sensor-captured depth maps are extensively noisy and incomplete, limiting their usefulness for fine-grained hand reconstruction. To bridge this gap, we propose GeoHand, a novel framework that unlocks high-quality geometric priors from a frozen foundational monocular geometry estimator (MoGe2). Recognizing that these priors are oriented toward general scenes, we introduce a map-level GeoAdapter to recalibrate the spatial features, specifically adapting them for detailed hand reconstruction. Furthermore, to systematically integrate these adapted priors without overwhelming intrinsic RGB appearance cues, we employ a gated cross-modal token fusion strategy. Finally, to secure precise local articulation, we design a Keypoint-Queried Iterative Refiner (KQIR) that uses projected joint locations to query geometry-aware image features for spatial correction. By combining global geometric disambiguation with local refinement in a unified pipeline, GeoHand achieves state-of-the-art performance on FreiHAND, DexYCB, and HO3Dv3, especially under severe occlusions and hand-object interactions.

</details>


### [115] [UniPPTBench: A Unified Benchmark for Presentation Generation Across Diverse Input Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17356)
*Bo Zhao,Maosheng Pang,Chen Zhang,Huan Yang,Yixin Cao,Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniPPTBench，一个针对演示文稿生成的统一基准测试，涵盖四种输入场景（模糊提示、长文档、多模态文档、多源生成），并配套了UniPPTEval场景感知评估协议，结合共享指标和场景特定指标，揭示了不同设置下性能差异显著，且通用质量指标无法反映特定场景下的任务完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演示文稿生成研究主要针对单一孤立输入设置，而实际应用场景多样（模糊提示、长文档、多模态材料、多源数据）。同时，现有评估缺乏场景特异性，仅依赖通用质量指标（如视觉吸引力、布局质量），无法评估各场景所需的核心能力（如接地压缩、视觉-文本对齐、跨源合成）。

Method: 构建UniPPTBench统一基准，覆盖四种代表性输入设置；提出UniPPTEval场景感知评估协议，包含共享指标（跨设置比较）和场景特定指标（针对各设置核心需求）；提供透明参考基线以支持可复现比较。

Result: 实验表明，不同设置间性能差异显著，存在内容接地、多模态集成和跨源合成等反复出现的失败模式；通用演示文稿质量指标表现良好并不一定意味着在接地场景中任务完成度也高。

Conclusion: UniPPTBench和UniPPTEval共同为跨真实场景的演示文稿生成评估提供了可靠且可诊断的基础。

Abstract: Existing works typically focus on presentation generation under isolated input settings, whereas real-world use cases span diverse scenarios, including vague user prompts, long documents, multimodal materials, and multiple heterogeneous sources. Moreover, current evaluations are often insufficiently scenario-specific. They mainly rely on generic presentation-quality criteria, such as visual appeal, layout quality, and overall coherence, but fail to assess the core capabilities required by different input settings, including grounded compression, visual-text alignment, and cross-source synthesis. Consequently, the field lacks a unified benchmark and a scenario-aware evaluation framework for faithfully diagnosing presentation-generation systems across diverse real-world settings. We present UniPPTBench, a unified benchmark for presentation generation across four representative input settings: vague-prompt, long-document, multimodal-document, and multi-source generation. We further introduce UniPPTEval, a scenario-aware evaluation protocol that combines shared metrics for cross-setting comparison with scenario-specific metrics tailored to the core requirements of each setting. We also provide transparent reference baselines to support reproducible comparison. Experiments on UniPPTBench reveal substantial performance variation across settings and recurring failure modes in content grounding, multimodal integration, and cross-source synthesis. In particular, strong performance on generic presentation-quality metrics does not necessarily imply strong task fulfillment in grounded scenarios. Together, UniPPTBench and UniPPTEval provide a faithful and diagnostic foundation for evaluating presentation generation across diverse real-world scenarios. Code and data will be publicly available.

</details>


### [116] [Omni-DuplexEval: Evaluating Real-time Duplex Omni-modal Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17360)
*Chaoqun He,Mingyang Xiang,Yingjing Xu,Bokai Xu,Junbo Cui,Jie Zhou,Yuan Yao,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对多模态大语言模型在实时双工交互场景下缺乏系统评测基准的问题，提出Omni-DuplexEval基准，包含实时描述和主动提醒两个场景、660个视频、9个任务，并引入基于LLM-as-a-Judge的自动评估框架。实验表明最佳模型仅达39.6%总体得分，主动提醒任务仅20.0%，揭示模型在平衡响应时效性与内容生成连贯性方面存在显著不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs主要在离线设置下评估（处理完整个视频后才生成响应），而实时双工交互场景（模型需持续处理流式输入并在适当时刻响应）缺乏全面的基准和自动评估方法。

Method: 构建Omni-DuplexEval基准，包含两个互补场景：实时描述（评估连续、时间对齐的响应生成能力）和主动提醒（评估识别显著事件并在适当时刻响应的能力）。包含660个人工标注视频，覆盖9个真实场景任务，采用开放式问题。提出基于LLM-as-a-Judge的自动评估框架，通过时间戳感知和顺序推理联合评估响应内容对齐和响应时机。

Result: 最佳模型在Omni-DuplexEval上总体得分仅39.6%，主动提醒任务仅20.0%。LLM-as-a-Judge评估与人工判断高度一致。

Conclusion: 当前实时双工MLLMs存在两大关键挑战：模型难以在及时响应与连贯、整体的内容生成之间取得平衡；模型常常无法同时确定何时响应以及生成什么内容。

Abstract: Real-time duplex interaction is essential for multimodal AI systems operating in real-world scenarios, where models must continuously process streaming inputs and respond at appropriate moments. However, most existing multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are evaluated in offline settings, where the entire video input is processed before any response is generated. While recent work has started to explore real-time duplex MLLMs, there is still no comprehensive benchmark or automatic evaluation method for this setting. To address this gap, we propose Omni-DuplexEval, a benchmark for systematically evaluating real-time duplex interaction. The benchmark consists of two complementary scenarios: (1) Real-Time Description, which evaluates the ability to generate continuous, time-aligned responses that track evolving multimodal inputs, and (2) Proactive Reminder, which evaluates the ability to identify salient events and respond at appropriate moments. Omni-DuplexEval contains 660 videos with fine-grained, human-annotated labels and precise temporal metadata, spanning 9 tasks grounded in real-world scenarios, where all questions are formulated as open-ended queries. We further introduce an automatic evaluation framework based on LLM-as-a-Judge, which enables systematic assessment by jointly evaluating response-content alignment and response timing through timestamp-aware and sequential reasoning, achieving strong alignment with human judgments. Experiments on state-of-the-art duplex MLLMs reveal substantial limitations. The best-performing model achieves only 39.6% overall, while scoring only 20.0% on Proactive Reminder. Our analysis identifies two key challenges: models struggle to balance timely responses with coherent, holistic content generation, and they often fail to determine both when to respond and what to produce. We hope our work facilitates further progress in MLLMs.

</details>


### [117] [Memory-Augmented Query Intent Understanding for Efficient Chat-based Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17365)
*Xianke Chen,Daizong Liu,Yushuo Lou,Xin Tan,Xun Yang,Shuhui Wang,Xun Wang,Jianfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MAQIU框架，通过轻量级记忆模块和记忆召回机制，在对话式图像检索中动态聚合和演化查询意图，并融合历史图像结果作为视觉引导，相比基线ChatIR减少了86.4%的对话编码计算量，同时性能显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式图像检索方法要么简单拼接历史查询导致计算冗余，要么依赖大语言模型重构当前查询导致意图表示不一致，亟需高效且语义连贯的意图更新机制。

Method: 提出MAQIU框架：1) 轻量级记忆模块动态聚合和演化跨轮次查询意图的语义表示；2) 记忆召回机制防止意图遗忘并增强长期语义完整性；3) 集成历史图像检索结果作为视觉引导，加强跨轮次关联并优化当前视觉理解。

Result: 在多项实验上取得显著性能提升，同时对话编码FLOPs相比基线ChatIR降低86.4%，保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: MAQIU通过高效的记忆机制有效解决了对话式图像检索中意图动态更新和语义一致性问题，为交互式检索提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Different from traditional text-to-image retrieval tasks, chat-based image retrieval allows the human-interactive system to iteratively clarify and refine user intent through multi-round dialogue, thereby achieving more fine-grained retrieval results. The key challenge in this task lies in dynamically understanding and updating the user's query intent across dialogue rounds. Although existing works have achieved great performance on this new task, they simply handle history query information either by directly concatenating all previous queries into a long textual sequence or by relying on large language models to reconstruct the current query from history. Such strategies are computationally redundant and easily lead to inconsistent intent representations as the dialogue progresses. To alleviate these issues, this paper proposes a novel and efficient memory-based user intent updating framework for the chat-based image retrieval task, called Memory-Augmented Query Intent Understanding (MAQIU). It introduces a lightweight memorization module that dynamically aggregates and evolves the semantic representation of query intent across dialogues, while a memory recall mechanism is further employed to prevent intent forgetting and enhance long-term semantic integrity. In addition, MAQIU also integrates historical image retrieval results as visual guidance, allowing the model to strengthen cross-round correlations and refine current visual understanding. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAQIU achieves substantial performance gains while maintaining high computational efficiency, reducing dialogue encoding FLOPs by 86.4\% compared with the prior baseline ChatIR. Source code is available at https://github.com/HuiGuanLab/MAQIU.

</details>


### [118] [Bridging Data Trials and Task Barriers: A Unified Framework for Sketch Biometric Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17367)
*Decheng Liu,Bin Hu,Xinbo Gao,Dawei Zhou,Chunlei Peng,Nannan Wang,Ruimin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了**素描生物特征识别**的新任务，旨在通过任务序列持续学习统一模型，解决跨模态与跨任务的联合挑战。研究设计了高效的大规模合成素描数据生成流水线，结合真实数据融合与可信样本回放技术，构建了统一框架，并建立了大规模基准数据集SketchUnified-BioID及相应评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态识别任务（如异质人脸识别、素描再识别）受限于真实素描数据稀缺、标注成本高、隐私风险大以及跨任务模型泛化能力不足等问题，缺乏一个能同时处理不同数据域和不同识别任务的统一框架。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含两大部分：1）高效合成素描生成流水线，大规模生成高质量合成人物和面部素描数据，降低成本和隐私风险，并通过融合真实数据增强鲁棒性；2）任务序列持续学习策略，模型先在人物数据集上完成素描行人再识别学习，随后通过可信样本回放技术保留已习得的人物识别能力，再无缝增量训练于面部数据集，使单一模型同时具备多种素描生物识别任务的跨任务能力。

Result: 构建了大规模基准数据集SketchUnified-BioID，并设计了多个实用的评估协议用于支持该新任务的研究。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架能够有效解决跨模态与跨任务的联合挑战，在降低数据成本和隐私风险的同时，使单一模型具备处理多种素描生物识别任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Different from existing cross-modality identification tasks (e.g., heterogeneous face recognition, sketch re-identification, etc.), we introduce a novel yet practical setting for these related identification tasks, named \textbf{sketch biometric identification}, which aims to continually train a unified model across different data domains, even diverse identification tasks. Sketch biometric identification faces challenges, including scarce real sketch data, high annotation costs, privacy risks, and insufficient generalization ability of cross-task models. Existing methods usually rely on limited real data or single-task optimization, making it difficult to effectively address the joint challenges of cross-modality and cross-task. This paper proposes a unified framework that integrates efficient synthetic sketch generation and task-sequential continual learning. First, we design an efficient pipeline to generate a large-scale and high-quality synthetic person and face sketch data, which significantly reduces costs and avoids privacy risks. Meanwhile, we enhance the model's robustness by fusing real data. Second, we construct a universal unified framework for sketch biometric identification, which adopts a task-sequential training strategy: the model first completes sketch person re-identification learning on the person dataset; subsequently, it maintains the acquired person recognition capability through a trusted sample replay technique and seamlessly performs incremental training on the face dataset. This enables a single model to simultaneously handle the cross-task capabilities of multiple sketch biometric identification tasks. To support the study of the mentioned sketch biometric identification, we built a new large-scale benchmark, SketchUnified-BioID, with several practical evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [119] [RadGenome-Anatomy: A Large-Scale Anatomy-Labeled Chest Radiograph Dataset via Physically Grounded Volumetric Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17368)
*Shuchang Ye,Mingyuan Meng,Hao Wang,Usman Naseem,Jinman Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RadGenome-Anatomy是最大的胸部X光片解剖标注数据集，包含2.5万多项研究中的2.1亿个解剖结构分割掩码。它通过CT体积的3D解剖掩码投影到2D放射空间构建，解决了传统2D标注的模糊性问题。基于该数据集训练的XAnatomy模型在心影增大、脊柱后凸和脊柱侧凸诊断中分别达到96.4%、95.6%和89.2%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片的解剖结构标注对医学图像分割和下游诊断任务至关重要，但直接在2D X光片上标注存在劳动密集、边界模糊、结构重叠遮挡等问题，导致现有数据集规模有限、解剖覆盖不全、标签可靠性不足。

Method: 1）利用大规模CT体积中的3D解剖掩码，通过规范放射摄影几何将其投影到2D放射空间，生成物理可解释的2D掩码；2）基于该数据集训练XAnatomy模型，预测结构特异性掩码并推导临床相关几何测量值。

Result: 构建了包含25,692项研究、210个解剖结构、超过1000万个分割掩码的数据集。XAnatomy模型在心影增大诊断中准确率96.4%，脊柱后凸95.6%，脊柱侧凸89.2%。

Conclusion: 通过从3D到2D的投影方式构建大规模解剖标注数据集，有效解决了2D X光片标注的模糊性问题，可支持基于几何测量的胸部X光片临床诊断，具有较高的诊断准确率。

Abstract: Anatomical structure labels for chest radiographs are essential for medical image segmentation and a broad range of downstream diagnostic tasks. However, annotating anatomy directly on 2D chest radiographs is labor-intensive and intrinsically ambiguous, as 3D anatomical structures are projected onto a single 2D plane where boundaries may overlap, be occluded, or appear only partially visible. Consequently, existing anatomy-labeled chest radiograph datasets remain limited in scale, anatomy coverage, and label reliability. To address these limitations, we introduce RadGenome-Anatomy, the largest anatomy-labeled chest radiograph dataset, containing over 10 million segmentation masks across 210 anatomical structures in 25,692 studies. It is constructed by projecting large-scale 3D anatomical masks from CT volumes into 2D radiographic space through canonical radiographic geometry. This shifts annotation from directly tracing uncertain 2D boundaries to defining anatomy in volumetric space, where structures that overlap or become partially invisible in radiographs remain spatially separable. As a result, each 2D mask represents the physically grounded projected footprint of a volumetrically defined structure. The scale and broad anatomical coverage of RadGenome-Anatomy, including structures that are overlapping, partially visible, or difficult to delineate directly, enable research on geometric measurements as explicit evidence for chest radiograph interpretation. We demonstrate this by training XAnatomy to predict structure-specific masks and derive clinically relevant measurements, achieving diagnostic accuracies of 96.4%, 95.6%, and 89.2% for cardiomegaly, kyphosis, and scoliosis, respectively.

</details>


### [120] [VISTA: Variance-Gated Inter-Sequence Test-Time Adaptation for Multi-Sequence MRI Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17433)
*Zhipeng Deng,Jiale Zhou,Wenhan Jiang,Haolin Wang,Xun Lin,Yafei Ou,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VISTA框架，通过序列间一致性探测和交叉视图分歧感知伪标签，解决多序列MRI分割中因模态交互偏移导致的域适应问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法仅处理单模态偏移，无法应对多序列MRI中破坏序列间一致性的模态交互偏移（dual-shift问题）。

Method: 1) ISIG：通过交换序列间的低频频谱和熵定位补丁生成一致性探测样本；2) CDPL：利用交叉视图分歧方差构建体素级可靠性度量，动态门控自训练并强制执行干预一致性。

Result: 在BraTS-GLI-Pre到BraTS-SSA和BraTS-PED的迁移中，Dice分别提升+1.89%和+2.82%，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: VISTA有效解决了多序列MRI的模态交互偏移问题，为临床环境中序列间一致性保持提供了新范式。

Abstract: Deploying multi-sequence magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) segmentation models to new clinical environments is challenging due to variations in scanners and acquisition protocols. Although existing TTA methods handle basic per-modality shifts, they often fail under a fundamental dual-shift problem, as their adaptation signals fail to capture modality-interaction shifts that disrupt inter-sequence consistency. To address this, we propose Variance-gated Inter-Sequence Test-time Adaptation (VISTA), a source-free framework that tackles modality-interaction shifts. First, we design an Inter-Sequence Intervention Generator (ISIG) that generates a set of consistency probes by swapping low-frequency spectra and entropy-localized patches across sequences, preserving anatomical semantics while challenging inter-sequence dependencies. Second, we introduce Cross-View Disagreement-Aware Pseudo Labeling (CDPL), which establishes a voxel-wise reliability metric using cross-view disagreement variance to dynamically gate self-training and enforce interventional consistency, encouraging the network to rely on robust anatomical semantics. Extensive experiments adapting from standard adult MRI (BraTS-GLI-Pre) to African low-field (BraTS-SSA) and pediatric (BraTS-PED) cohorts show improved performance over competing methods under clinical shifts, achieving absolute Dice improvements of +1.89% (SSA) and +2.82% (PED) over the source model. The code is available at https://github.com/dzp2095/VISTA.

</details>


### [121] [Medical Context Distorts Decisions in Clinical Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17436)
*David Restrepo,Ira Ktena,Maria Vakalopoulou,Stergios Christodoulidis,Enzo Ferrante*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了视觉-语言模型（VLM）在临床决策支持中的可靠性，识别出三种失败模式：对文本模态的过度依赖、对无关临床病历的虚假关联以及对语义等价输入的提示敏感性。通过在MIMIC-CXR数据集上的系统性实验，发现VLMs在胸片任务中决策主要由文本主导，即使视觉证据可用；模型易受无关报告影响，且微小提示变化可推翻正确的图像预测。研究强调在临床部署前需建立明确的安全保障和压力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLMs被越来越多地用于临床决策支持，但其在需要融合视觉和文本信息的真实临床场景中的可靠性尚未被充分表征，缺乏对潜在失败模式的系统性识别和分析。

Method: 在MIMIC-CXR胸部X光数据集上，对通用域和医学微调的开源及闭源VLMs进行系统性评估，通过操纵图像-文本对齐、临床病史和提示表述方式，分析模型的决策行为。

Result: 发现了三种失败模式：VLMs决策被文本模态主导，即使视觉证据可用；模型受无关临床报告影响显著；微小提示变化可推翻正确的基于图像的预测。

Conclusion: 在考虑将VLMs用于临床实践之前，亟需建立明确的安全保障机制和压力测试方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly proposed for clinical decision support, yet their reliability in real-world scenarios that require integrating both visual and textual context from medical records remains poorly characterized. This paper identifies three failure modes: (1) modality over-reliance on text over images, (2) spurious reliance on irrelevant clinical history, and (3) prompt sensitivity across semantically equivalent inputs. We evaluate a diverse set of general-domain and medically-tuned open and closed VLMs on chest x-ray tasks using MIMIC-CXR. By systematically manipulating image-text alignment, clinical history, and prompt formulations, we found that VLM decisions are dominated by the text modality, even when visual evidence is available. Moreover, we observed that VLMs are heavily influenced by irrelevant reports, while minor prompt changes can reverse correct image-based predictions. Our findings underscore the need for explicit safeguards and stress-testing before considering the use of these models in clinical practice.

</details>


### [122] [FastOCR: Dynamic Visual Fixation via KV Cache Pruning for Efficient Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17447)
*Zihan Tang,Leqi Shen,Hui Chen,Ao Wang,Ben Wan,Yan Feng,Ke Zhang,Sicheng Zhao,Tongxuan Liu,Guiguang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FastOCR框架，通过动态视觉聚焦现象，在不永久丢弃视觉token的前提下，仅保留5%的视觉token进行每步解码，实现OCR任务上3倍注意力加速且保持98%的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在OCR文档图像上失效，因为文档图像密集且每个token可能对应字符或结构元素，永久丢弃会导致灾难性精度下降。

Method: 基于动态视觉聚焦现象，提出训练无关的FastOCR框架，包含两个模块：(1) 聚焦引导剪枝，识别少量聚焦层并在每步选择最任务相关的视觉token；(2) 跨步注视重用，利用注视点逐步移动特性，从上一步热启动当前步。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL上仅关注5%的视觉token，保持98%的准确率，注意力延迟降低3.0倍；在五种不同架构和规模的VLM上均有效。

Conclusion: 动态调整每步关注的token而非永久驱逐，可避免信息永久丢失，实现OCR任务的高效加速。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have shown strong promise on Optical Character Recognition (OCR), yet the sheer number of visual tokens required to encode dense documents incurs prohibitive inference cost. Existing pruning methods rely on physical eviction, e.g., permanently discarding visual tokens during the prefill stage. While effective for natural images, this strategy fundamentally breaks down on OCR, where virtually every visual token may correspond to a character or structural element, and any irreversible loss leads to catastrophic accuracy degradation. We observe that, although document images appear globally dense and seemingly unprunable, the model's attention to them is in fact temporally sparse: at each decoding step it concentrates on a small region that shifts gradually across steps, much as a human reader fixates on successive words rather than perceiving an entire page at once. Motivated by this Dynamic Visual Fixation phenomenon, we recast the intractable global pruning problem as a tractable local, dynamic one and propose FastOCR, a training-free framework with two complementary modules. Specifically, Focal-Guided Pruning identifies a small set of focal layers and selects the most task-relevant visual tokens from them at each step, while Cross-Step Fixation Reuse exploits the gradual shift of fixation to warm-start each step from the previous one. By dynamically adjusting which tokens are attended rather than evicting any from the cache, FastOCR avoids permanent information loss. Extensive experiments show that FastOCR serves as a plug-and-play acceleration module, generalizing consistently across five VLMs of varying sizes and architectures. On Qwen2.5-VL, FastOCR retains 98% of the unpruned model's accuracy while attending to only 5% of the visual tokens per decoding step, reducing attention latency by 3.0$\times$.

</details>


### [123] [DeTrack: A Benchmark and Altitude-Aware Dual World Model for Drone-embodied Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17451)
*Guyue Hu,Haoming Liu,Siyuan Song,Chenglong Li,Feng Chen,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了DeTrack，一种无人机具身跟踪任务，要求无人机在交互式3D环境中通过在线自我中心观测和主动飞行控制闭环跟踪目标。同时构建了包含11,368条目标轨迹的大规模基准，并提出了AaDWorlds（高度感知双世界模型框架），通过伪高度感知观测和想象未来状态缓解目标可见性与飞行安全之间的矛盾，实验证明其有效提升闭环跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中目标跟踪基准主要基于被动2D视频序列（固定摄像头或预定航线），无人机被视为被动相机而非具身智能体，缺乏在动态3D场景中主动感知、交互和控制运动的能力。

Method: 提出了DeTrack任务定义和AaDWorlds框架：1) 高度感知模块；2) 双世界模型，分别想象高/低空条件下的未来状态；3) 结合伪高度感知观测与想象未来状态进行闭环控制。

Result: 在包含11,368条轨迹的大规模DeTrack基准上，AaDWorlds在所有评估指标上均提升了闭环跟踪性能。

Conclusion: AaDWorlds通过高度感知双世界模型有效缓解了目标可见性与飞行安全之间的内在矛盾，为无人机具身跟踪提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aerial object tracking has broad applications in public safety, emergency rescue, wildlife monitoring, and related fields. However, existing aerial tracking benchmarks are mainly based on passive 2D video sequences captured from fixed camera locations or predefined flight paths, where drones are treated as passive cameras rather than embodied agents that actively perceive, interact, and control their motion in dynamic 3D scenes. In this paper, we define a new drone-embodied tracking task, termed DeTrack, which requires a drone to track a target in interactive 3D environments using online egocentric observations and active flight control in a closed loop. We build a large-scale benchmark containing 11,368 target trajectories across diverse scenes, rendering conditions, semantic regions, and moving distractors, together with evaluation metrics for target visibility, tracking accuracy, and trajectory success. We further propose AaDWorlds, an altitude-aware dual world model framework for drone-embodied tracking. AaDWorlds consists of an altitude-aware perception module and dual world models that imagine future states under both high- and low-altitude regimes. By combining pseudo altitude-aware observations and imagined future states, AaDWorlds alleviates the intrinsic altitude-mediated contradiction between target visibility and flight safety. Experiments on the DeTrack benchmark demonstrate that AaDWorlds improves closed-loop tracking performance across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [124] [EchoSR: Efficient Context Harnessing for Lightweight Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17470)
*Hanli Zhao,Binhao Wang,Shihao Zhao,Tao Wang,Kaihao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EchoSR 是一个面向轻量级图像超分的高效上下文利用框架，通过解耦多尺度感受野建模与层级上下文融合，在多个基准上以约2倍速度超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级超分方法在扩展感受野时存在计算开销大、核大小简单放大、缺乏多尺度融合机制等问题，难以在重建 fidelity 与计算效率间取得良好平衡。

Method: 提出 EchoSR 框架，包含高效上下文利用策略（将特征学习解耦为局部、多尺度、全局建模三个阶段）和跨尺度重叠融合机制（促进跨尺度无缝集成）。

Result: 在多个基准上一致优于 SOTA 轻量级超分方法，同时推理速度提升约2倍。

Conclusion: EchoSR 通过解耦多尺度建模与层级融合，有效兼顾了轻量级超分的重建质量与计算效率，具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Image super-resolution (SR) aims to reconstruct high-quality, high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) inputs and plays a critical role in various downstream applications. Despite recent advancements, balancing reconstruction fidelity and computational efficiency remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in resource-constrained scenarios. While existing lightweight methods attempt to expand receptive fields, many of them either incur substantial computational overhead, naively scale up kernel sizes, or lack mechanisms for coherent multi-scale integration, limiting their overall effectiveness and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose EchoSR, an efficient context-harnessing framework for lightweight image super-resolution, which unifies multi-scale receptive field modeling and hierarchical context fusion. EchoSR decouples feature learning into disentangled local, multi-scale, and global modeling stages through an efficient context-harnessing strategy, and further promotes seamless cross-scale integration via a cross-scale overlapping fusion mechanism. Extensive experiments have shown that EchoSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art lightweight super-resolution methods across multiple benchmarks, while also achieving a faster speed $(\sim 2\times)$. The source code is available at \url{https://github.com/funnyWang-Echoes/EchoSR}.

</details>


### [125] [Weighted Reverse Convolution for Feature Upsampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17472)
*Wentong Li,Zhiyuan Qi,Zichen Zhao,Kai Zhang,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出加权反卷积（WRC），将特征上采样视为逆问题，通过空间自适应加权Tikhonov正则化最小二乘公式，高效生成密集的高质量视觉特征，在多个下游任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视觉基础模型（VFM）的patch级特征本质上是粗粒度的，限制了其在需要细粒度定位、密集预测和点对应等任务上的效果，因此需要有效的特征上采样方法。

Method: 将特征上采样建模为加权Tikhonov正则化最小二乘问题，引入空间变化权重调节数据保真度和先验强度，提出加权反卷积（WRC）作为空间自适应逆算子，并利用闭式FFT解保持高效可微性。

Result: 集成到轻量级自监督稠密化框架中，在分割、深度估计、视频目标分割、目标发现、关键点对应等多个下游基准上一致提升了密集特征质量，同时保持高计算效率。

Conclusion: WRC作为一种即插即用的上采样算子，能够有效克服过平滑问题，保留关键结构，显著提升VFM特征的密集化质量。

Abstract: Pre-trained vision foundation models (VFMs) provide strong semantic representations, yet their patch-level features are inherently coarse, limiting their effectiveness on tasks requiring fine-grained localization, dense prediction, and point-wise correspondence. In this work, we revisit feature upsampling for VFMs from the perspective of \textbf{\textit{inverse problem}} and propose Weighted Reverse Convolution (WRC), a spatially adaptive inverse operator for densifying high-level visual descriptors. Specifically, we formulate feature upsampling as a weighted Tikhonov-regularized least-squares problem, where spatially varying weights modulate both data fidelity and prior strength at each spatial location. This allows WRC to adapt the reconstruction to spatially varying feature characteristics, thereby preserving critical structures while mitigating over-smoothing. Moreover, WRC retains an efficient, fully differentiable closed-form FFT solution, making it a practical drop-in upsampling operator. Integrated into a lightweight self-supervised densification framework, WRC consistently improves dense feature quality across various downstream benchmarks, including segmentation, depth estimation, video object segmentation, object discovery, and keypoint correspondence, while maintaining high computational efficiency.

</details>


### [126] [Mamba-VGGT: Persistent Long-Sequence Video Geometry Grounded Transformer via External Sliding Window Mamba Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17478)
*Tianchen Deng,Zhenxiang Xiong,Nailin Wang,Fangjinhua Wang,Jiuming Liu,Jianfei Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mamba-VGGT，通过滑动窗口Mamba（SWM）记忆模块和零初始化空间记忆注入器，解决VGGT在长序列3D重建中的几何遗忘和累积漂移问题，实现线性复杂度的长程几何推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGGT在长序列3D场景重建中因全局注意力二次复杂度而被迫使用截断时间窗口，导致灾难性的几何遗忘和累积漂移，亟需一种能保持长程几何一致性的高效方法。

Method: 提出Sliding Window Mamba (SWM)记忆模块，利用选择性状态空间模型在时间窗口间显式维护外部记忆token，蒸馏并传播全局几何先验；同时提出Zero-Init Spatial Memory Injector，通过零卷积层将持久记忆自适应融合到patch token流中，保证结构稳定和特征对齐。

Result: 在保持空间一致性和减少轨迹累积误差方面显著优于现有VGGT方法，实现了线性复杂度的长程几何推理。

Conclusion: Mamba-VGGT为大规模3D环境中的几何基础世界建模提供了一种可扩展的线性复杂度解决方案，有效克服了Transformer在长序列几何重建中的内存约束。

Abstract: Visual Geometry Grounded Transformers (VGGT) have set new benchmarks in high-fidelity 3D scene reconstruction. However, as the sequence length increases, these models suffer from catastrophic geometric forgetting and accumulation drift, primarily due to the quadratic complexity of global attention which necessitates truncated temporal windows. To overcome the resulting geometric drift, we present Mamba-VGGT, an enhanced VGGT framework capable of persistent long-range reasoning. Our key contribution is a Sliding Window Mamba (SWM) memory module that maintains an explicit external memory token across temporal windows. This module leverages selective state-space modeling to distill and propagate global geometric priors, effectively bypassing the memory constraints of traditional transformers. To integrate these long-term temporal cues without disrupting the highly optimized spatial features of the pre-trained VGGT, we propose a Zero-Init Spatial Memory Injector. Utilizing zero-convolutional layers, this injector adaptively fuses persistent memory into the patch token stream, ensuring structural stability and seamless feature alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms existing VGGT-based methods in maintaining spatial consistency and reducing trajectory accumulation errors. Our work provides a scalable, linear-complexity solution for geometry-grounded world modeling in extensive 3D environments.

</details>


### [127] [Omni-Customizer: End-to-End MultiModal Customization for Joint Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17488)
*Yuheng Chen,Qingdong He,Teng Hu,Yuji Wang,Yabiao Wang,Lizhuang Ma,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Omni-Customizer，一个端到端的联合音视频定制生成框架，通过Omni-Context Fusion模块、Masked TTS Cross-Attention机制和Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE等技术，实现多模态身份信息（视觉身份与语音音色）的精确绑定与融合，在双模态定制生成任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的音视频联合生成研究缺乏对跨多个交互主体的视觉身份和语音音色同时进行连贯的多模态定制，即实现多模态身份信息的精准绑定与无缝融合尚未被充分探索。

Method: 1) Omni-Context Fusion (OCF)模块：用密集多模态身份线索丰富基础文本提示；2) Masked TTS Cross-Attention (MTP-CA)机制：防止"语音泄漏"问题；3) Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE (SA-MRoPE)：将视觉/音频参考token及TTS嵌入锚定到对应的语义描述；4) 训练策略：交错音视频调度适配多语言场景，渐进式课程学习（in-pair到cross-pair）促进高层身份特征学习。

Result: 在双模态定制生成任务上达到SOTA性能，在视觉身份相似度、音色一致性、音视频同步精度和整体视音频保真度方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: Omni-Customizer有效解决了多模态身份信息的联合定制生成难题，为同时保留多交互主体的视觉身份和语音音色提供了可行的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: The landscape of joint audio and video generation has been fundamentally transformed by the advent of powerful foundation models. Despite these strides, achieving cohesive multimodal customization for the simultaneous preservation of visual identities and vocal timbres across multiple interacting subjects remains largely underexplored. To bridge this gap, we present Omni-Customizer, an end-to-end framework targeted at the precise binding and seamless fusion of multimodal identity information. Specifically, we introduce an Omni-Context Fusion (OCF) module that effectively enriches the base textual prompt with dense, multimodal identity cues, along with a Masked TTS Cross-Attention (MTP-CA) mechanism explicitly designed to prevent the severe "speech leakage" problem. Within this architecture, we propose Semantic-Anchored Multimodal RoPE (SA-MRoPE) to anchor visual and audio reference tokens, along with TTS embeddings, to their corresponding semantic descriptions, enabling structured multimodal fusion and robust identity binding. Furthermore, we devise a comprehensive training strategy that incorporates interleaved audio-video scheduling to rapidly adapt the audio branch to multilingual scenarios without degrading foundational priors, and a progressive in-pair to cross-pair curriculum to facilitate the learning of high-level and robust identity features. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Omni-Customizer achieves state-of-the-art performance in dual-modal customized generation, excelling across visual identity similarity, timbre consistency, precise audio-video synchronization, and overall video-audio fidelity.

</details>


### [128] [Employing Vision-Language Models for Face Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17489)
*Erdi Sarıtaş,Eren Onaran,Vitomir Štruc,Hazım Kemal Ekenel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索使用现成的视觉-语言模型（VLM）在零样本设置下进行人脸图像质量评估（FIQA），旨在解决传统方法缺乏可解释性的问题。通过多维度评估框架（包括生物特征效用、一致性、鲁棒性）和多样化数据集分析，发现VLM的性能主要依赖于架构而非参数量，且零样本FIQA评分有潜力作为传统FIQA管线的可解释性补充模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FIQA方法虽具有高效用，但作为“黑盒”仅输出标量分数，缺乏人类可理解的解释，限制了其在需要可操作反馈的人机协同场景（如自动边境控制）中的有效性。因此，作者希望利用现成的VLM来弥合这一差距，实现兼具可解释性的零样本FIQA。

Method: 1) 构建了全面的VLM性能评估框架；2) 通过错误-拒绝曲线（ERC）对传统FIQA方法进行基准测试；3) 使用多样化数据集（包括监控场景和合成生成数据）分析VLM的可解释性、一致性和对提示词的鲁棒性；4) 进行合成消融实验研究参数量与性能的关系。

Result: 1) VLM的生物特征效用性能主要取决于架构而非参数量；2) 大多数VLM的输出与传统方法一致；3) VLM的排序性能和生成分数会随提示词变化而变化；4) 合成消融实验表明，增大参数量可提升内部一致性，但在退化检测性能上不如小模型。

Conclusion: 零样本FIQA评分估计具有前景，能够有效作为传统FIQA管线的可解释性补充模块。代码已开源。

Abstract: Face Image Quality Assessment (FIQA) is a crucial control step in biometric pipelines. It ensures only reliable samples are processed to maintain system accuracy. State-of-the-art FIQA methods achieve high utility but typically operate as "black boxes." They produce scalar scores without human-interpretable justifications. This lack of transparency limits their effectiveness in human-in-the-loop scenarios, such as automated border control, where actionable feedback is essential. In this paper, we investigate the potential of off-the-shelf Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to bridge this gap by performing FIQA in a zero-shot setting. We present a comprehensive evaluation framework for assessing VLM performance. This involves benchmarking traditional FIQA methods through error-versus-reject curves. Additionally, using a diverse set of datasets, ranging from surveillance-oriented to synthetically generated, we analyzed their interpretability, consistency, and robustness to prompt changes. Our results show biometric utility performance depends significantly on architecture, not merely on parameter count. Most VLMs' outputs align with those of traditional methods. We also find that VLM ranking performance and the generated scores may vary across prompts. Our synthetic ablation study shows that while increasing the parameter count can improve internal consistency, it yields worse degradation-detection performance than smaller models. These findings suggest that zero-shot FIQA score estimation using VLMs is promising and could effectively complement conventional FIQA pipelines as an interpretability module. The codes are available at https://github.com/ThEnded32/VLM4FIQA.git.

</details>


### [129] [A Distributional View for Visual Mechanistic Interpretability: KL-Minimal Soft-Constraint Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17504)
*Guancheng Zhou,Yisi Luo,Zhengfu He,Zhenyu Jin,Xuyang Ge,Wentao Shu,Deyu Meng,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布视角的视觉机械可解释性新范式，通过KL最小化优化问题建模，解决了以往方法在可解释性与忠实性之间的偏差，并利用能量引导的扩散后验采样实现了理论与实践的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉机械可解释性方法多依赖启发式方法（如top-K激活检索或正则化优化），缺乏理论视角。作者建立了分布化视角，揭示以往方法存在统计偏差：要么对人工不可解释（偏离自然图像分布），要么对模型不忠实（无法激活模型特征）。

Method: 1. 建立分布化视角，将特征激活对自然图像分布的影响建模为KL最小化优化问题；2. 提出KL最小化软约束原则，从理论上平衡可解释性与忠实性；3. 通过能量引导的扩散后验采样实现该原则。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提分布化视角的理论合理性，并在DINOv3视觉模型上证明了该范式的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 分布化视角为视觉MI提供了统一的理论框架，KL最小化软约束原则有效解决了可解释性与忠实性之间的权衡问题，能量引导扩散采样是一种可行的实现方案。

Abstract: Most current paradigms in visual mechanistic interpretability (MI) remain confined to interpreting internal units of the vision model via heuristic methods (e.g., top-$K$ activation retrieval or optimization with regularization). In this work, we establish a theoretical distributional view for visual MI, which models the influence of a feature activation on the natural image distribution, thereby formulating a Kullback-Leibler (KL)-minimal optimization problem to model the MI task. Under this framework, statistical biases are identified within previous MI paradigms, which reveal that they may either be perceptually uninterpretable to humans (i.e., deviate from the natural image distribution), or mechanistically unfaithful to the vision models (i.e., unable to activate model features). To resolve the biases under the distributional view, we propose a model with a KL-minimal soft-constraint principle for visual MI that theoretically balances interpretability and faithfulness. We realize this principle via energy-guided diffusion posterior sampling. Extensive experiments validate the theoretical soundness of the proposed distributional view and demonstrate the practical effectiveness of our paradigm on the DINOv3 vision model.

</details>


### [130] [Degradation Frequency Curve: An Explicit Frequency-Quantified Representation for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17506)
*Xinghua Huang,Zhixiong Yang,Chen Wu,Shengxi Li,Shuaifeng Zhi,Yue Zhang,Qibin Hou,Xin Deng,Jingyuan Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出退化频率曲线（DFC），一种频域结构化表示方法，将视觉上纠缠的退化效应转化为可测量的退化坐标空间，并基于此开发了DFC引导的图像修复器（DFC-IR），在全能盲图像修复任务上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全能盲图像修复方法将退化视为隐含因子而非可测量、可操控的显式目标，在混合、复合或未见退化条件下性能受限。

Method: 提出DFC量化频域中逐频带残差与退化图像的能量比，将退化效应转化为可测量的坐标空间；DFC可自适应分解为频带级谱标记作为可重用修复先验；基于此构建DFC-IR，一种标记条件化的多尺度框架，从中间修复结果逐步估计DFC并指导退化感知修复。

Result: 在标准、复合、未见和真实退化基准上均取得SOTA性能，泛化能力显著提升。

Conclusion: DFC为全能图像修复提供了有效的表示基础，在复杂退化场景下表现出优异的性能和泛化性。

Abstract: A fundamental difficulty in all-in-one blind image restoration is that degradation is usually treated as an implicit factor hidden in degraded-to-clean mapping, rather than as an explicit object that can be measured and manipulated. This limitation becomes more pronounced under mixed, compound, or unseen degradation conditions, where degradation effects are hard to assign to predefined labels or task-specific parameters. We propose the Degradation Frequency Curve (DFC), a structured spectral representation that quantifies degradation responses by measuring band-wise residual-to-degraded energy ratios in the frequency domain. DFC converts visually entangled and hard-to-describe degradation effects into a measurable degradation coordinate space. Moreover, DFC can be adaptively decomposed into band-wise spectral tokens, allowing local degradation responses to be represented as reusable restoration priors. Based on this representation, we develop the DFC-guided Image Restorer (DFC-IR), a token-conditioned multi-scale framework that progressively estimates DFCs from intermediate restorations and uses the resulting spectral tokens to guide degradation-aware restoration in a coarse-to-fine manner. Extensive experiments on standard, composite, unseen, and real-world degradation benchmarks show that DFC provides an effective representation basis for all-in-one restoration, leading to state-of-the-art performance and improved generalization under complex degradation profiles.

</details>


### [131] [Designing streetscapes from street-view imagery using diffusion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17527)
*Yuzhou Chen,Yuebing Liang,Lingqian Hu,Kailai Sun,Qingqi Song,Chang Zhao,Shenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散模型的生成式多模态AI框架，能够根据目标视觉指标（如绿化率、天空视域指数等）合成替代性街景图像，支持城市规划中的场景探索。实验表明，该框架生成的街景具有高真实感和语义一致性，视觉控制优于文本控制，在芝加哥和奥兰多数据集上mIoU提升最高达46.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有街景图像研究主要聚焦于量化当前城市环境指标（如绿化率、天空视域指数等），但缺乏生成替代性、非现有城市场景的能力，而这是城市规划和设计等地理空间学科的核心任务。

Method: 构建多模态数据集（街景图像+文本描述+分割图+道路掩码+视觉指标量化值），基于扩散模型实现以文本和视觉条件共同控制的街景生成，并进行定量评估（LPIPS、mIoU等指标）。

Result: 加入视觉控制后LPIPS降低约6%，mIoU在奥兰多提升23.7%、芝加哥提升46.4%，部分建筑视域指数提升超100%；文本与视觉控制冲突时，视觉控制主导生成结果。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了街景生成的重要基准，证明生成式AI可作为实用、可扩展、可控的城市场景探索方法，并揭示了视觉控制在城市场景生成中的支配地位和进一步发展需求。

Abstract: Street-view imagery (SVI) is widely used to quantify key indicators of urban environment, such as green- ery, sky, or road view indices. However, existing studies largely focus on measuring current streetscapes and rarely support the generation of alternative and non-existing urban scenarios, which is a core task in geospatial disciplines such as urban planning and design. To address this gap, we propose a gener- ative multimodal AI framework that synthesizes alternative streetscapes conditioned on targeted visual metrics, enabling direct visual exploration of urban scenarios. We first construct a multimodal dataset that aligns SVIs with textual descriptions, segmentation maps, road masks, and quantitative metrics of visual elements in Chicago and Orlando. Using this dataset, we demonstrate that diffusion models can produce realistic and semantically consistent streetscape imagery while responding to both textual and imagery controls. Our quantitative evaluations show that incorporating visual controls can improve semantic consistency, reducing the LPIPS index by approximately 6% while maintaining global visual realism. In addition, overall semantic consistency increases by 23.7% in Orlando and 46.4% in Chicago, as measured by the mIoU index, with class-wise gains exceeding even 100% improvement for building view indices. Streetscape generation can be controlled in a fine-grained manner by both visual and textual prompts, and when textual and visual controls conflict, imagery controls consistently dominate, indicating a clear control hierarchy and the importance of further developing visual controls for urban scene generation. Overall, this work establishes an important benchmark for streetscape generation us- ing SVIs and diffusion models, and illustrates how generative AI can serve as a practical, scalable, and controllable approach for urban scenario exploration.

</details>


### [132] [$\textit{Don't Guess, Just Ask}$: Resolving Ambiguity in Referring Segmentation via Multi-turn Clarification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17531)
*Yuting Yang,Haichao Jiang,Tianming Liang,Quan Zhang,Jian-Fang Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IC-Seg框架，通过多轮对话主动澄清用户意图来解决模糊查询下的指代分割问题，并引入Hi-GRPO分层优化策略提升对话效率，在模糊查询和标准推理分割基准上均取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代分割方法假设用户查询已足够精确清晰，这在真实场景中不成立；用户可能提供模糊、歧义的查询，导致模型随意猜测用户意图而产生错误分割结果。

Method: 提出IC-Seg智能体框架，通过多轮对话主动澄清用户意图后再进行分割；提出Hi-GRPO分层优化策略，在轨迹、轮次和步骤三个层级注入密集监督信号，激励高效意图澄清，消除冗余交互并提升对话质量。

Result: 在提出的Ambi-RVOS模糊查询视频指代分割基准上大幅超越现有方法，同时在标准推理分割基准上保持最优性能。

Conclusion: IC-Seg有效解决了模糊查询下的指代分割难题，证明了主动意图澄清在多模态分割中的重要价值，且不影响标准场景下的性能。

Abstract: Referring segmentation aims to segment the target objects in images or videos based on the textual query. Despite remarkable progress over the past years, existing works always assume that the user-provided queries are already precise and clear. However, this assumption is impractical. In real-world scenarios, it is unrealistic to expect all users to thoroughly review their visual content and carefully ensure their queries are unique and unambiguous. When encountering such cases, existing segmentation models tend to arbitrarily guess the user preferences, often resulting in undesired outcomes. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{IC-Seg}, a novel agentic framework that proactively clarifies user intent through multi-turn conversation before segmentation. To effectively incentivize this capability, we further introduce \textbf{Hi-GRPO}, a new hierarchical optimization strategy that injects dense and informative supervision signals at the trajectory, turn, and step levels. This strategy encourages efficient intent clarification, effectively eliminating redundant interactions and improving overall dialogue quality. For evaluation, we establish \textbf{Ambi-RVOS}, a referring video object segmentation benchmark with ambiguous user queries. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IC-Seg not only outperforms existing methods by a large margin in resolving ambiguous queries, but also maintains state-of-the-art performance on standard reasoning segmentation benchmarks. Code and data will be released at \url{https://github.com/iSEE-Laboratory/IC-Seg}.

</details>


### [133] [HL-OutPaint: Coarse-to-Fine Video Outpainting for High-Resolution Long-Range Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17543)
*Jeongeun Park,Janghyeok Han,Geonung Kim,Hyun-Seung Lee,Kyuha Choi,Youngseok Han,Sunghyun Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HL-OutPaint，一种面向长序列的高分辨率视频外扩框架，采用粗到细的两阶段策略，通过全局-局部帧交换机制构建全局粗引导(GCG)，在统一表示中编码长期结构一致性和短期时间动态，实现大空间外扩与长序列的稳定连贯生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频外扩方法大多只能处理大空间外扩或长时间序列中的一个挑战，且缺乏显式的全局时空一致性保障机制，导致生成结果存在明显局限。

Method: 采用粗到细的两阶段流水线：1) 通过新颖的全局-局部帧交换机制构建GCG（低分辨率表示），耦合稀疏全局关键帧与局部时间窗口并在采样过程中交换信息；2) 在GCG引导下进行高分辨率视频外扩，生成空间细节丰富且时间一致的内容。

Result: 在涉及大空间外扩和长视频序列的挑战性场景中，HL-OutPaint优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过将全局结构建模与细粒度合成分离，该框架能够稳定、连贯地生成大空间扩展和长视频序列的外扩结果。

Abstract: Video outpainting generates plausible visual content beyond the original spatial extent of a video, playing a key role in adapting videos to diverse display formats. To support such use cases, it must enable large spatial extrapolation over long sequences. However, most existing methods address only one of these challenges or lack explicit mechanisms for ensuring global spatio-temporal consistency, leading to notable limitations. In this paper, we propose HL-OutPaint, a high-resolution video outpainting framework for long sequences. Our approach follows a coarse-to-fine strategy with a two-stage pipeline. We first construct Global Coarse Guidance (GCG), a low-resolution representation that captures global structure and dominant motion across the video. Unlike naive downsampling, GCG is built via a novel global-local frame swapping mechanism that couples sparse global keyframes with local temporal windows and exchanges information during sampling. This enables GCG to encode both long-term structural consistency and short-term temporal dynamics in a unified representation. Guided by this representation, HL-OutPaint then performs high-resolution outpainting to generate spatially detailed and temporally consistent content. By separating global structure modeling from fine-grained synthesis, our framework achieves stable, coherent generation for large spatial expansion and long video sequences. Extensive experiments show that HL-OutPaint outperforms existing methods in challenging scenarios involving wide spatial extrapolation and long video sequences.

</details>


### [134] [Stable Routing for Mixture-of-Experts in Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17571)
*Zirui Guo,Quan Cheng,Da-Wei Zhou,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出StaR-MoE，一种针对可扩展混合专家模型（MoE）在类增量学习（CIL）中的路由稳定框架。通过敏感性感知路由对齐和非对称容量正则化，同时实现旧类路由稳定和新类容量有效利用，在四个CIL基准上取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE方法在CIL中扩展专家时，路由器会因专家扩展产生路由漂移，将旧类样本重新分配给新专家，即使旧专家被冻结也会干扰已有专家组成，破坏了旧知识的保留。

Method: 提出StaR-MoE，包含两个互补组件：(1) 敏感性感知路由对齐，通过敏感性引导的约束将当前旧类路由行为与历史路由分布对齐；(2) 非对称容量正则化，在扩大专家池的同时鼓励有效利用，不破坏类别特定的路由特化。

Result: 在四个标准CIL基准上的大量实验表明，StaR-MoE在平均准确率和最后准确率上均一致优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 路由稳定性在可扩展MoE的CIL中至关重要，StaR-MoE通过路由级框架有效解决了专家扩展带来的路由漂移问题，实现了旧知识保留与新类适应的平衡。

Abstract: Class-incremental learning (CIL) requires models to learn new classes sequentially while preserving prior knowledge. Recently, approaches that combine pre-trained models with mixture-of-experts (MoE) have received increasing attention in CIL: they typically expand experts during learning and employ a router to assign weights across experts. However, existing MoE methods often overlook routing drift induced by expert expansion. Once new experts are introduced, the router may reassign samples from earlier classes to newly added experts, thereby perturbing previously established expert compositions and causing interference even when old experts remain frozen. We argue that expandable MoE in CIL requires two complementary properties: stable old-class routing for knowledge preservation and sufficient capacity utilization for new-class adaptation. To this end, we propose Stable Routing for MoE (StaR-MoE), a routing-level framework for expandable MoE in CIL. By incorporating sensitivity-aware routing alignment, StaR-MoE aligns current old-class routing behavior with historical routing distributions through sensitivity-guided constraints. Complementarily, StaR-MoE introduces asymmetric capacity regularization to encourage effective utilization of the expanded expert pool without compromising class-specific routing specialization. Extensive experiments across four standard CIL benchmarks demonstrate that StaR-MoE consistently improves both average and last accuracy over state-of-the-art methods, highlighting the importance of stable routing.

</details>


### [135] [TAME: Test-Time Adversarial Prompt Tuning via Mixture-of-Experts for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17577)
*Xin Wang,Yixu Wang,Jiaming Zhang,Ruofan Wang,Jiaqi Yu,Kai Chen,Jingjing Chen,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAME是一种测试时防御方法，通过输入条件化的混合专家（MoE）框架替代单自适应提示，在保持CLIP零样本泛化能力的同时，显著提升其对抗鲁棒性（AutoAttack下至少49.1%提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模预训练视觉语言模型（如CLIP）在零样本泛化上表现优异，但对难以察觉的对抗扰动高度脆弱，存在开放世界部署的安全隐患。现有方法需要下游任务重训练，缺乏高效的测试时防御手段。

Method: TAME在TAPT基础上进行架构重构：用输入条件化的混合专家（MoE）框架替代单一自适应提示，维护一组可学习的专家提示，并通过依赖输入的路径机制为每个测试样本聚合定制提示组合。防御由三个无监督目标驱动：多视图预测熵最小化、层间视觉令牌统计量对齐预计算的干净/对抗参考分布、以及MoE正则化（平衡专家利用率和提示多样性）。

Result: 在ImageNet及10个零样本数据集上，TAME使原始CLIP在AutoAttack下的零样本对抗鲁棒性提升至少49.1%，同时保持干净样本上的泛化能力；相较于现有对抗提示调优方法，平均鲁棒性提升至少30.2%。

Conclusion: TAME通过MoE架构实现更具表达力和适应性的测试时防御，有效兼顾了对抗鲁棒性与零样本泛化能力，为视觉语言模型的安全部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large-scale pre-trained Vision-Language models (VLMs), such as CLIP, exhibit strong zero-shot generalization, yet remain highly vulnerable to imperceptible adversarial perturbations, raising serious safety concerns for open-world deployment. To enhance robustness without requiring downstream task-specific retraining, we propose TAME, a novel test-time defense. Building upon our prior Test-Time Adversarial Prompt Tuning (TAPT), TAME introduces an architectural reformulation by replacing TAPT's single adaptive prompt with an input-conditioned Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework, enabling more expressive and adaptive defense. Specifically, TAME maintains a bank of learnable expert prompts and employs an input-dependent routing mechanism to aggregate a customized prompt mixture for each unlabeled test sample at inference time. This test-time defense mechanism is driven by three unsupervised objectives: (1) multi-view prediction entropy minimization, (2) layer-wise alignment of visual token statistics to precomputed clean and adversarial reference distributions, and (3) MoE regularization for balanced expert utilization and prompt diversity. We evaluated TAME on 11 benchmark datasets, including ImageNet and 10 additional zero-shot datasets. The results show that TAME improves the zero-shot adversarial robustness of the original CLIP by at least 49.1% under AutoAttack while largely preserving generalization on clean samples. TAME also consistently outperforms existing adversarial prompt tuning methods across multiple prompt designs, yielding an average robustness gain of at least 30.2%.

</details>


### [136] [AgentSteerTTS: A Multi-Agent Closed-Loop Framework for Composite-Instruction Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17583)
*Bin Kang,Shaoguo Wen,Yang Fan,Shunlong Wu,Junjie Wang,Yulin Li,Junzhi Zhao,Junle Wang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentSteerTTS提出了一种多智能体闭环框架，通过对抗解耦、双流锚定控制和快慢反馈机制，实现了对复合指令的忠实表达能力控制，解决了文本意图与声学实现之间的结构不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS模型虽具高表现力，但在复合指令的细粒度控制上存在困难，根本原因是离散文本意图与连续声学实现之间的结构不匹配。

Method: 提出AgentSteerTTS多智能体框架：1) 对抗解耦智能体通过泄漏抑制正则化分离说话人身份和情感韵律子空间；2) 双流锚定控制器包含检索智能体选择表达锚点和合成智能体通过门控注意力融合为连续控制向量；3) 快慢反馈智能体通过潜在梯度修正优化输出强度，并用高层感知评判解决语义-声学不匹配。

Result: 在复合指令基准和公开测试集上，AgentSteerTTS相较于基线方法取得了一致且显著的改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的多智能体闭环框架有效实现了对复合指令的意图忠实表达控制。

Abstract: While existing text-to-speech (TTS) models exhibit high expressiveness, fine-grained control over composite instructions remains challenging due to the structural mismatch between discrete textual intents and continuous acoustic realizations. Inspired by human cognitive decoupling, we introduce AgentSteerTTS, a multi-agent closed-loop framework designed for intent-faithful expressive control of composite instructions. First, in our framework, an adversarial disentanglement agent mitigates speaker-emotion leakage by learning separable identity and emotion-prosody subspaces with leakage-suppressing regularization. Next, a Dual-Stream Anchoring Controller grounds abstract intents using a large-scale acoustic prototype library: a Retrieval Agent selects expressive anchors, while a Synthesis Agent fuses them into continuous control vectors via gated attention. Finally, a Fast-Slow Feedback Agent refines output intensity through latent gradient correction and resolves semantic-acoustic mismatches using high-level perceptual critique. Experiments on a composite-instruction benchmark and public test sets show that AgentSteerTTS yields consistent and significant improvements to the baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [137] [VVitCutLER: Towards Unsupervised Object Detection and Segmentation in Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17584)
*Zhijing Lu,Khurram Azeem Hashmi,Didier Stricker,Muhammad Zeshan Afzal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VVitCutLER是一个无监督视频目标检测与实例分割框架，通过提出时间稳定的伪标签生成器VitCut（利用跨帧区域一致性和蒸馏解码器）以及跨帧特征聚合，有效减少了运动模糊、遮挡等场景下的时间漂移和伪标签闪烁问题，在多个视频基准上显著提升了检测与分割性能并降低了时间不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督像素级视频理解在真实场景中面临运动模糊、遮挡和快速目标动态所导致的时间漂移和伪标签闪烁问题，现有方法缺乏有效的时序一致性约束。

Method: 提出VVitCutLER框架，核心贡献是VitCut——一个时间稳定的伪标签生成器，通过跨帧区域一致性减少误差累积，并利用蒸馏解码器进行实例掩码预测；在此基础上集成跨帧特征聚合以增强视频级鲁棒性。

Result: 在标准视频基准上，VVitCutLER显著提升了检测和分割性能，同时降低了时间不稳定性。

Conclusion: 时序一致的监督信号对于鲁棒的像素级视频理解至关重要。

Abstract: Unsupervised pixel-level video understanding remains challenging in real-world scenarios, where motion blur, occlusion, and fast object dynamics often cause temporal drift and flickering pseudo-labels.We propose VVitCutLER, an unsupervised framework for video object detection and instance segmentation, which improves the quality of pseudo-labels through temporal consistency. Our core contribution is VitCut, a temporarily stable pseudo-label generator that reduces error accumulation during field degradation through cross-frame region consistency. Meanwhile, VitCut uses a distillation decoder to achieve effective instance mask prediction. Then, based on VitCut, VVitCutLER further integrates cross-frame feature aggregation to enhance video-level robustness. Extensive experiments on standard video benchmarks demonstrate that VVitCutLER significantly improves detection and segmentation performance while reducing temporal instability. These results highlight the importance of temporally consistent supervision for robust pixel-level video understanding.

</details>


### [138] [Error-Decomposed Class-Conditional Fusion for Statistically Guaranteed Hard-Category Robust Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17591)
*Guowei Luo,Ziqi Shi,Zhao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出错误分解类条件融合框架（ED-CCF），通过四态错误分类的动态校准机制，在不影响全局稳定性的前提下，显著提升了操作关键场景中脆弱少数类（如cz类）的检测性能（mAP50提升22.4%），并以严格的统计检验验证了方法的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统聚合检测指标掩盖了关键长尾少数类中灾难性且重复的失败模式，现有方法无法在严格协议下同时修复脆弱类别与保持稳定类别性能，存在Hard-Category Reliability Problem (HCRP)这一根本性架构挑战。

Method: 提出Error-Decomposed Class-Conditional Fusion (ED-CCF)决策层推理框架，将预测投影到四态错误分类体系（quad-state error taxonomy）中，仅在严格的实证依据下动态激活校准路径，避免启发式全局后处理。

Result: 在600张图像验证集上，将脆弱类cz的mAP50从0.089343提升至0.109353（+22.4%），全局mAP50从0.581925提升至0.584864，保持Pareto最优；50组配对试验中96%胜率，Bonferroni校正Wilcoxon检验p<0.05。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了输出级融合作为可审计、有统计保障的安全关键视觉感知范式，为严格修复脆弱类别而不牺牲稳定类别性能提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Aggregate object detection metrics inherently mask catastrophic and repeatable failures in operationally critical, long-tail minority classes. This paper formally defines this pervasive vulnerability as the Hard-Category Reliability Problem (HCRP): the fundamental architectural challenge of strictly rectifying vulnerable categories without compromising the performance boundaries of stable classes under stringent protocols. To systematically dismantle this limitation, we propose Error-Decomposed Class-Conditional Fusion (ED-CCF), an elegant decision-layer inference framework. Diverging from heuristic global post-processing, ED-CCF projects predictions into a sophisticated quad-state error taxonomy, dynamically activating calibration pathways exclusively upon rigorous empirical justification. On a highly constrained 600-image validation benchmark, isolating cz as the critical vulnerability (HCEC=0.86, BSR=0.14), our framework achieves a targeted breakthrough: it elevates cz mAP50 from 0.089343 to 0.109353 (a massive +22.4% relative surge) while flawlessly preserving the Pareto optimality of global stability (raising all mAP50 from 0.581925 to 0.584864). Backed by exhaustive validation across 50 paired subset trials demonstrating an overwhelming 96% win rate and strict Bonferroni-corrected Wilcoxon significance (p<0.05), this work fundamentally redefines output-level fusion as an auditable, statistically guaranteed paradigm for safety-critical visual perception.

</details>


### [139] [SafeLens: Deliberate and Efficient Video Guardrails with Fast-and-Slow Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17610)
*Shahriar Kabir Nahin,Hadi Askari,Muhao Chen,Anshuman Chhabra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SafeLens提出了一种快慢推理架构的视频护栏框架，通过影响引导过滤仅保留2.4%的高质量数据并增强测试时链式思维推理，在显著降低推理成本的同时，在真实与AI生成视频基准上超越开源和闭源模型，实现最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频护栏方法对所有输入统一使用大型视觉语言模型，导致高推理成本和计算资源低效分配，缺乏根据输入复杂度动态分配计算量的机制。

Method: 提出快慢推理架构（fast-and-slow inference）实现输入自适应的计算成本分配；通过影响引导过滤（influence-guided filtering）从SafeWatch数据集中仅保留2.4%的高质量数据；在过滤后数据上增强结构化链式思维（Chain-of-Thought）轨迹，实现测试时推理（test-time reasoning）。

Result: 在真实世界和AI生成视频基准上达到最先进性能，优于SafeWatch-8B、OmniGuard-7B等开源模型以及GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-pro等闭源模型，同时显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 高效的设计比单纯扩展数据或模型规模更有效，验证了通过智能计算分配和测试时推理可以兼顾性能与效率。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online video platforms and AI-generated content has made reliable video guardrails a key challenge for safety and real-world deployment. While most videos can be screened through fast pattern recognition, a small subset requires deeper reasoning over temporally complex content and nuanced policy constraints. Existing approaches typically rely on large vision-language models applied uniformly across all inputs, resulting in high inference costs and inefficient allocation of computation. We propose SafeLens, a video guardrail framework that introduces a fast-and-slow inference architecture for efficient and accurate content moderation with variable computational cost across inputs. Additionally, we construct a high-quality dataset by applying influence-guided filtering to the SafeWatch Dataset, retaining only 2.4% of the original data. To further address limitations of training-time scaling, we enable test-time reasoning by augmenting the filtered data with structured Chain-of-Thought traces. Across real-world and AI-generated video benchmarks, SafeLens achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming strong open-source video guardrails (e.g., SafeWatch-8B, OmniGuard-7B) and closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4, Gemini-3.1-pro) while significantly reducing inference cost, demonstrating that efficient design serves to be more effective than scaling data or model size alone.

</details>


### [140] [SynVA: A Modular Toolkit for Vessel Generation and Aneurysm Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17620)
*Marten J. Finck,Niklas C. Koser,Sarker M. Mahfuz,Tameem Jahangir,Jon E. Wilhelm,Daniel Behme,Naomi Larsen,Wojtek Palubicki,Sylvia Saalfeld,Sören Pirk*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynVA是一个模块化工具包，用于合成血管网格和生成解剖一致的颅内动脉瘤，通过流匹配生成健康血管、基于学习的方法生成动脉瘤，并发布了5万个带标签的网格样本数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内动脉瘤是导致中风和致命性出血的主要原因，随着人口老龄化，脑血管疾病负担增加。数字孪生和深度学习的有效性受限于大规模高质量医学数据及其标签的稀缺性。

Method: 提出SynVA模块化工具包，包含：(1)基于流匹配的健康血管网格生成方法；(2)基于学习的解剖条件动脉瘤网格生成方法（动脉瘤从已有血管几何计算而非孤立生成）；(3)基于生理学原理和统计先验的SynVA程序化模型，用于大规模数据集生成。

Result: 定量和定性评估表明SynVA能生成逼真的血管几何和解剖合理的动脉瘤。不同方法各有所长：某些方法生成的动脉瘤形状更符合专家感知，另一些方法在定量相似度指标上表现更好。

Conclusion: SynVA通过合成数据解决了医学影像中数据稀缺问题，可生成5万个带标签网格样本用于下游视觉任务，为大规模脑血管疾病分析提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Intracranial aneurysms (IAs), characterized by unpredictable growth and risk of rupture, are a major cause of stroke and can lead to life-threatening hemorrhages with high mortality and long-term disability. With aging populations, the incidence and overall burden of cerebrovascular diseases are expected to increase, highlighting the need for scalable approaches to analyze complex medical data and improve population-level understanding of these conditions. While digital twins and deep learning offer promising avenues for improving diagnosis, prognosis, and treatment, their effectiveness is limited by the scarcity of large-scale, high-quality medical data and corresponding labels. We present Synthetic VAsculature (SynVA), a modular toolkit for vascular mesh generation and anatomically consistent aneurysm synthesis. SynVA combines novel flow-matching-based methods for generating healthy vessel meshes with learning-based approaches for anatomy-conditioned aneurysm mesh generation - aneurysms are computed from pre-existing vascular geometries rather than being generated in isolation. In addition, we introduce the SynVA procedural model for vascular and aneurysm synthesis based solely on physiological principles and statistical priors, which enables the generation of large-scale datasets (e.g., for the training of mesh-based generative models). To this end, we release a dataset of 50,000 fully labeled mesh samples for a variety of downstream vision tasks, such as semantic segmentation. Extensive quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that SynVA generates realistic vessel geometries and anatomically plausible aneurysms. Specifically, our experiments indicate that some methods produce aneurysm shapes more aligned with expert human perception while others perform better on quantitative similarity metrics with reconstructions of real aneurysms.

</details>


### [141] [Multi-task learning on partially labeled datasets via invariant/equivariant semi-supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17624)
*Miquel Martí i Rabadán,Alessandro Pieropan,Hossein Azizpour,Atsuto Maki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了不变性和等变性半监督学习（FixMatch和Dense FixMatch）在多任务部分标注数据场景下的表现，在Cityscapes和BDD100K数据集上进行目标检测和语义分割实验，结果表明该方法在少量标注样本时显著优于监督基线，等变性方法效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多任务模型在部分标注数据集上训练时面临的挑战，即不同任务输出结构不同且标注数据不完整的问题。

Method: 采用FixMatch（不变性半监督学习）及其等变性扩展Dense FixMatch，在Cityscapes和BDD100K数据集上对目标检测和语义分割任务进行实验，并考虑不同标注子集大小和重叠度。

Result: 两种半监督方法在大多数情况下优于监督基线，当标注样本越少时改进越显著，等变性方法（Dense FixMatch）整体表现更好。

Conclusion: 不变性/等变性学习是解决有限标注数据下多任务学习的一个有前景的通用方向。

Abstract: We investigate the potential of invariant and equivariant semi-supervised learning for addressing the challenges of training multi-task models on partially labeled datasets with differently structured output tasks. Specifically, we use the popular FixMatch method for invariant semi-supervised learning and its equivariant extension Dense FixMatch. We evaluate their performance on the Cityscapes and BDD100K datasets in the context of the prevalent object detection and semantic segmentation tasks in computer vision. We consider varying sizes of the subsets annotated for each task and different overlaps among them. Our results for both invariant and equivariant semi-supervised learning outperform supervised baselines in most situations, with the most significant improvements observed when fewer labeled samples are available for a task and generally better results for the latter approach. Our study suggests that invariant/equivariant learning is a promising general direction for multi-task learning from limited labeled data.

</details>


### [142] [SegRAG: Training-Free Retrieval-Augmented Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17630)
*Abderrahmene Boudiaf,Irfan Hussain,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SegRAG提出一种免训练的检索增强分割框架，通过从DINOv3特征库中提取类别特定点提示来增强SAM3，在开放词汇分割任务上显著提升性能，尤其在零样本域迁移场景中将mIoU从25.27提升至59.24。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割模型（如SAM3）依赖概念级文本提示，但当目标类别在预训练数据中视觉表征不足或外观偏离典型范式时性能下降，文本提示无法提供空间信号来解决这种歧义。

Method: 1) 离线阶段：使用冻结的DINOv3 ViT-L/16从标注参考图像提取patch级描述符，通过类内凝聚蒸馏(ICCD)筛选可靠原型；2) 推理阶段：拓扑相似性定位(TSG)计算查询图像与原型间的余弦相似度图，通过连通分量分析和高置信度区域非极大值抑制提取峰值点作为点提示；3) 将点提示与类名文本联合输入SAM3进行掩码解码。

Result: 在四个开放词汇基准上持续优于SAM3文本基线，LVIS上mIoU提升+3.92；在AgML农业基准（零样本域迁移）上将mIoU从25.27提升至59.24（+33.97），部分类别从0恢复至95+ mIoU。

Conclusion: ICCD、TSG和联合提示各自独立贡献且组合后效果叠加，SegRAG无需任务特定训练和合成数据，是一种有效的免训练增强方案。

Abstract: Here's a trimmed version under 1920 characters:
  Open-vocabulary segmentation models such as SAM3 achieve strong performance through concept-level text prompting, yet degrade when the target class is visually underrepresented in pretraining data or when its appearance departs from canonical depictions. Text prompts provide no spatial signal to resolve such ambiguity. We present SegRAG, a training-free retrieval-augmented segmentation framework that grounds SAM3 with spatially precise, class-specific point prompts derived from a curated DINOv3 feature bank. During an offline stage, patch-level descriptors are extracted from annotated reference images using a frozen DINOv3 ViT-L/16 backbone and filtered by Intra-Class Cohesion Distillation (ICCD), retaining only prototypes that reliably retrieve within-class foreground. At inference, Topographic Similarity Grounding (TSG) computes a cosine-similarity landscape between the query image and retrieved prototypes, identifies spatially coherent high-confidence regions via connected-component analysis, and extracts peak locations through non-maximum suppression. These point prompts are delivered to SAM3 alongside the class-name text in a single joint grounding pass, enabling the mask decoder to resolve semantic intent and spatial evidence together. SegRAG requires no task-specific training and no synthetic data. On four open-vocabulary benchmarks it achieves consistent gains over the SAM3 text-only baseline, with improvements of up to +3.92 mIoU on LVIS. On AgML agricultural benchmarks representing a zero-shot domain transfer setting, it raises mean IoU from 25.27 to 59.24 (+33.97) and recovers individual classes from zero to over 95 mIoU. Ablation studies confirm that ICCD, TSG, and joint prompting each contribute independently and compound when combined. Code is available at https://github.com/boudiafA/SegRAG.

</details>


### [143] [SparseSAM: Structured Sparsification of Activations in Segment Anything Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17633)
