We formulate functionalization as a graph completion problem over a new functional graph representation, where labeled nodes represent object parts, labeled edges encode functional and contact relations, and movable nodes carry motion attributes, so that structural functional deficiencies manifest as missing nodes or incorrect edges. We develop a neural Graph Functionalizer (GraFu) to complete an incomplete graph representing a non-functional 3D object. The completed graph then drives a geometry realization stage that instantiates predicted connectors and structural elements in 3D, with the compelling side effect of rectifying erroneous human-annotated and predicted motions. To support training and evaluation, focusing on furniture as a rich and challenging target category, we introduce FurFun-233, a dataset of 233 paired non-functional and functionalized furniture models. On PartNet-Mobility ("zero-shot") and HSSD test sets, our method matches state-of-the-art methods in motion prediction accuracy while substantially improving functionality in terms of collision and connectivity.

</details>


### [180] [SAS: Semantic-aware Sampling for Generative Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18012)
*Mingzhuo Li,Guang Li,Linfeng Ye,Jiafeng Mao,Takahiro Ogawa,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种语义感知的数据集蒸馏方法，利用CLIP作为语义先验，通过三种语义评分函数和两阶段采样策略，从现有蒸馏方法生成的图像池中筛选出语义上类区分性强且多样性的紧凑数据集，在多个数据集和模型上取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法主要关注匹配数据分布或下游训练统计量，忽略了蒸馏数据中高层语义信息的保留，导致生成的紧凑数据集在语义区分性和多样性方面不足。

Method: 利用CLIP作为语义先验，设计三种语义评分函数（类相关性、类间可分性、集内多样性）；提出两阶段采样策略：第一阶段过滤语义区分性高的样本构建候选集，第二阶段进行动态多样性感知选择以减少冗余并保持语义覆盖。

Result: 在多个数据集、图像池和下游模型上的大量实验表明，该方法在性能上取得一致性提升，验证了将语义信息融入数据集蒸馏的有效性。

Conclusion: 引入语义感知视角能够有效提升蒸馏数据集的类判别性和多样性，为数据集蒸馏提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Deep neural networks have achieved impressive performance across a wide range of tasks, but this success often comes with substantial computational and storage costs due to large-scale training data. Dataset distillation addresses this challenge by constructing compact yet informative datasets that enable efficient model training while maintaining downstream performance. However, most existing approaches primarily emphasize matching data distributions or downstream training statistics, with limited attention to preserving high-level semantic information in the distilled data. In this work, we introduce a semantic-aware perspective for dataset distillation by leveraging Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) as a semantic prior for post-sampling. Our goal is to obtain distilled datasets that are not only compact but also semantically class-discriminative and diverse. To this end, we design three semantic scoring functions that quantify class relevance, inter-class separability, and intra-set diversity in a pretrained semantic space. Based on image pools generated by existing distillation methods, we further develop a two-stage strategy for effective sampling: the first stage filters semantically discriminative samples to form a reliable candidate set, and the second stage performs a dynamic diversity-aware selection to reduce redundancy while preserving semantic coverage. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, image pools, and downstream models demonstrate consistent performance gains, highlighting the effectiveness of incorporating semantic information into dataset distillation.

</details>


### [181] [TinySAM 2: Extreme Memory Compression for Efficient Track Anything Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18013)
*Zhaoyuan Ding,Yijing Yang,Han Shu,Xinghao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TinySAM 2 提出了一种轻量化视频分割模型，通过内存质量管理、联合时空令牌压缩和轻量级编码器，在仅使用7%内存令牌和3%训练数据的情况下，达到SAM 2.1 90%的性能，显著降低了部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM 2在视频分割中性能优异，但其多阶段图像编码器和记忆模块的计算复杂度较高，导致模型在实际应用中的部署门槛过高。为解决这一问题，作者提出TinySAM 2，旨在平衡性能与效率。

Method: 1) 引入内存质量管理机制，选择性保留高信息量的历史帧作为记忆；2) 提出联合时空令牌压缩：空间域使用平均池化压缩冗余令牌，时间域基于令牌级相似度度量从记忆库中选择信息令牌；3) 采用RepViT作为轻量级图像编码器，进一步减少模型参数。

Result: 在DAVIS和SA-V等数据集上，TinySAM 2达到SAM 2.1 90%的性能，仅使用7%的内存令牌和3%的训练数据。

Conclusion: 该研究有效缓解了SAM 2在参数量、计算负载和部署成本方面的瓶颈，为视频分割模型在设备上的广泛应用提供了一种资源高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM 2) serves as a core foundation model in the field of video segmentation. Building upon the original SAM model, it introduces a memory bank mechanism and demonstrates outstanding performance in tasks such as semi-supervised video object segmentation and tracking anything. However, the complex computational characteristics of SAM 2's multi-stage image encoder and memory module have raised the barrier to the model's deployment in practical applications. To address this issue, we propose TinySAM 2, a lightweight video segmentation model that balances performance and efficiency. First, a memory quality management mechanism is introduced to select and retain high-informative historical frames as the memory. In addition, a joint-spatial-temporal token compression is proposed that reduces the memory storage and computational cost. Specifically, average pooling is employed to first compress redundancy tokens in the spatial domain. In the temporal domain, informative tokens are selected across frames in the memory bank based on token-level similarity measurement. Besides, we take RepViT as the lightweight image encoder, which further reduces the model parameters. Extensive experiments on challenging datasets such as DAVIS and SA-V demonstrate that TinySAM 2 achieves 90% of the performance of SAM 2.1, with only 7% memory tokens and 3% training data. This study effectively alleviates the bottlenecks in parameter count, computational load, and deployment costs associated with SAM 2, providing a resource-efficient solution for the widespread application of video segmentation models on devices.

</details>


### [182] [See What I Mean: Aligning Vision and Language Representations for Video Fine-grained Object Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18018)
*Boyuan Sun,Bowen Yin,Yuanming Li,Xihan Wei,Qibin Hou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SWIM是一种仅需文本提示即可实现细粒度物体理解的训练策略，通过训练时使用掩码监督对齐跨模态注意力，解决了多模态大语言模型中属性词与物体名词注意力分布不一致的问题，在细粒度理解基准上超越了基于视觉提示的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要显式视觉提示（如掩码或点）才能实现细粒度物体理解，而预训练多模态大语言模型中存在系统性偏差：属性词产生尖锐的局部化激活，物体名词因语义引用偏差和分布式高级表征产生弥散、分散的注意力模式，导致文本-视觉对齐不足。

Method: 提出SWIM训练策略：（1）构建NL-Refer数据集，为每个物体掩码配对精确的自然语言指代表达；（2）提取物体名词的多层跨注意力图，并与真实掩码强制空间一致性约束；（3）仅在训练阶段使用掩码监督引导跨模态注意力，推理时仅需文本提示。

Result: SWIM显著提升了文本-视觉对齐质量，在细粒度物体理解基准上超越了基于视觉提示的方法。

Conclusion: 通过训练阶段引入掩码监督来对齐物体名词的注意力分布，SWIM实现了无需显式视觉提示的细粒度物体理解，验证了跨模态注意力对齐的有效性。

Abstract: We present SWIM (See What I Mean), a novel training strategy that aligns vision and language representations to enable fine-grained object understanding solely from textual prompts. Unlike existing approaches that require explicit visual prompts, such as masks or points, SWIM leverages mask supervision only during training to guide cross-modal attention, allowing the model to automatically attend to the user-specified object at inference. Our cross-attention analysis of pretrained multimodal large languagemodels (MLLMs) reveals a systematic discrepancy: Attribute words produce sharp, localized activations in the visual modality, whereas object nouns yield diffuse and scattered patterns due to semantic reference bias and distributed high-level representations. To address this misalignment, we construct NL-Refer, an enriched dataset, in which each object mask is paired with a precise natural language referring expression. SWIM extracts multi-layer cross-attention maps from object nouns and enforces spatial consistency with ground-truth masks. Experimental results demonstrate that SWIM substantially improves text-visual alignment and achieves superior performance over visual-prompt-based methods on fine-grained object understanding benchmarks. The code and data are available at \href{https://github.com/HumanMLLM/SWIM}{https://github.com/HumanMLLM/SWIM}.

</details>


### [183] [DSAA: Dual-Stage Attribute Activation for Fine-grained Open Vocabulary Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18023)
*Donghong Jiang,Endian Lin,Hanqing Liu,Mingjie Liu,Luoping Cui,Zhao Yang,Chuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出双阶段属性激活（DSAA）框架，通过属性前缀适配器（APA）和键值调制器（K/V Modulator）分别在文本嵌入和BERT编码阶段增强属性语义，并引入属性感知对比损失，以解决开放词汇目标检测模型中属性信息被类别信号边缘化导致的细粒度检测性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇目标检测（OVD）模型在涉及颜色、材质、纹理等属性的细粒度检测任务中表现不佳，其根本原因是类别信号主导推理过程，导致属性信息被边缘化，造成属性与目标对象的错误绑定。

Method: 提出DSAA框架：1）在文本嵌入阶段，使用属性前缀适配器（APA）注入显式属性先验；2）在BERT编码阶段，通过K/V调制器选择性增强属性令牌的键和值向量；3）引入属性感知对比损失，提升训练时同类别不同属性实例的区分能力。

Result: 在FG-OVD基准上，该方法在多种主流开放词汇模型中均取得了有效性验证。

Conclusion: DSAA框架通过双阶段属性增强和属性感知对比损失，有效缓解了OVD模型中类别信号对属性信息的压制，显著提升了细粒度开放词汇检测性能。

Abstract: Open-Vocabulary Object Detection (OVD) models break the limitations of closed-set detection, enabling the iden- tification of unseen categories through natural language prompts. However, they exhibit notable limitations in fine- grained detection tasks involving attributes like color, ma- terial, and texture. We attribute this performance bottle- neck in OVD models to a core issue: when category sig- nals dominate, OVD models tend to marginalize attribute information during inference. This leads to incorrect bind- ing between attributes and target objects. To address this, we propose the Dual-Stage Attribute Activation (DSAA) framework, which enhances fine-grained detection capa- bilities by strengthening attribute semantics at two criti- cal stages. In the text embedding stage, we employ At- tribute Prefix Adapter (APA) module to generate attribute prefixes that inject explicit attribute priors. To further am- plify the influence of these attributes, our Key/Value (K/V) Modulator module then intervenes during the BERT encod- ing phase, selectively enhancing the Key and Value vec- tors of the corresponding attribute tokens. In addition, we introduce an attribute-aware contrastive loss to improve discrimination among same-category instances with differ- ent attributes during training. Experimental results on the FG-OVD benchmark demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across various mainstream open-vocabulary mod- els.

</details>


### [184] [SGSoft: Learning Fused Semantic-Geometric Features for 3D Shape Correspondence via Template-Guided Soft Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18039)
*Soyeon Yoon,Chang Wook Seo,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SGSoft提出了一种统一的内在管道方法，通过构建测地对应场、学习多模态密集描述符并在描述符空间中执行最近邻搜索，实现了变形3D形状间的密集对应，在跨类别泛化、精度-效率权衡和近实时推理方面达到了最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形3D形状间的密集对应因结构可变性、非等距变形和拓扑不一致而长期存在挑战，现有方法在泛化性、几何保真度和效率之间难以兼顾。

Method: (i) 在规范模板上构建测地对应场；(ii) 利用预训练语义先验监督学习多模态密集描述符；(iii) 通过描述符空间中的最近邻搜索在单次前向传播中检索密集对应。

Result: 在跨类别泛化上达到最优，实现了最佳精度-效率权衡，无需预对齐、成对优化或后细化即可实现近实时推理。

Conclusion: 学习到的描述符可有效迁移到语义分割和变形迁移等下游任务，为密集3D对应建立了一个可扩展且可部署的范式。

Abstract: Learning dense correspondences across deformable 3D shapes remains a long-standing challenge due to structural variability, non-isometric deformation, and inconsistent topology. Existing methods typically trade off generalization, geometric fidelity, and efficiency. We address this by proposing SGSoft, a unified intrinsic pipeline that (i) constructs a geodesic correspondence field on a canonical template, (ii) learns multimodal dense descriptors guided by pretrained semantic priors with this geodesic correspondence field supervision, (iii) retrieves dense correspondences in a single feed-forward pass via nearest-neighbor search in descriptor space. This formulation enables stable and topology-invariant supervision under large pose variation, structural differences, and remeshing. SGSoft achieves state-of-the-art inter-category generalization while offering the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off among prior methods. It also achieves near real-time inference without pre-alignment, pairwise optimization, or post-refinement. Learned descriptors can be transferred effectively to downstream tasks such as semantic segmentation and deformation transfer, establishing a scalable and deployment-ready paradigm for dense 3D correspondence.

</details>


### [185] [OmniSelect: Dynamic Modality-Aware Token Compression for Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18041)
*Morunliu Yang,Ruotao Xu,Le Li,Yue Wang,Jianxin Zhang,Juntao Li,Yihang Lou,Siwei Feng,Peifeng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniSelect提出了一种无需训练、模态自适应的令牌剪枝框架，通过AudioCLIP评估跨模态相关性，动态选择音频中心、视频中心或均匀剪枝策略，实现对多模态输入的高效令牌压缩，在保持性能的同时显著降低计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OmniLLM处理长多模态令牌序列时计算开销大，而已有的令牌压缩方法依赖固定的、模态特定的指导策略，无法根据不同查询中模态重要性的变化进行自适应调整，存在"一刀切"的局限。

Method: 1) 利用轻量级AudioCLIP模型估计跨模态相关性；2) 根据相关性得分将输入划分为三种剪枝模式：音频中心、视频中心和均匀剪枝；3) 在每个时间组内进行细粒度令牌剪枝，自适应分配剪枝比例以保留信息丰富的令牌。

Result: 实验表明OmniSelect在实现高效多模态令牌缩减的同时保持了强劲的性能，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模模态偏好并实现动态策略选择，OmniSelect有效避免了固定压缩策略的弊端，为OmniLLM提供了一种训练免费、模态自适应的令牌压缩方案。

Abstract: Omnimodal large language models (OmniLLMs) have recently gained increasing attention for unified audio-video understanding. However, processing long multimodal token sequences introduces substantial computational overhead, making efficient token compression crucial. Existing methods typically rely on fixed, modality-specific guidance, which fails to account for the varying importance of modalities across different queries. To address this limitation, we propose $\textbf{OmniSelect}$, a training-free, modality-adaptive token pruning framework that dynamically selects appropriate compression strategies for multimodal inputs. Specifically, we leverage a lightweight AudioCLIP model to estimate cross-modal relevance and categorize each input into three pruning regimes: Audio-Centric, Video-Centric, and Uniform pruning. Based on these relevance scores, OmniSelect further performs fine-grained token pruning within each temporal group, adaptively allocating pruning ratios to preserve informative tokens across modalities. By explicitly modeling modality preference and enabling dynamic strategy selection, OmniSelect effectively avoids the pitfalls of one-size-fits-all compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves efficient multimodal token reduction while maintaining strong performance, without requiring any additional training.

</details>


### [186] [Efficient 3D Content Reconstruction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18052)
*Jiahao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本论文从生成与重建两条互补路线推进自动3D内容创作：提出Instant3D实现5-20秒内从文本/图像生成高质量3D资产，以及FastMap实现比先前最优方法快10倍的运动恢复结构管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 替代传统劳动密集型的3D建模和扫描流程，实现从文本或图像直接合成/恢复3D资产，以加速游戏、VR、机器人及仿真领域的资产制作、交互世界生成和训练数据收集。

Method: 生成方面：Instant3D结合多视图扩散模型和前馈稀疏视图3D重建；重建方面：FastMap采用一阶优化与融合GPU内核，实现结构光流管道的加速。

Result: Instant3D可在5-20秒内生成高质量3D资产；FastMap在保持姿态精度和新视图合成质量相当的前提下，实现较先前最优方法高达10倍的加速。

Conclusion: 在3D内容生成与重建两个互补范式上均取得显著进展，推动了自动3D内容创建的效率和质量边界。

Abstract: Automatic 3D content creation seeks to replace labor-intensive modeling and scanning pipelines with systems that can synthesize or recover 3D assets directly from text or images. Its applications span video games, virtual reality, robotics, and simulation, enabling rapid asset prototyping, diverse interactive world generation, and efficient 3D data collection for training foundation models. Contemporary solutions largely follow two complementary paradigms: (i) text- or image-to-3D generation, which learns priors over 3D geometry and appearance to create novel assets from natural language or a single view image; and (ii) 3D reconstruction, which estimates camera poses and geometry from RGB images. This thesis advances both directions. On the generation side, I introduce Instant3D, which combines multi-view diffusion with feed-forward sparse-view 3D reconstruction to produce high-quality assets in 5-20 seconds. On the reconstruction side, I develop FastMap, a structure-from-motion pipeline that achieves up to 10x speedup over prior state-of-the-art by using first-order optimization with fused GPU kernels extensively, while maintaining comparable pose accuracy and downstream novel view synthesis quality.

</details>


### [187] [Threats to Arabic Handwriting Recognition: Investigating Black-Box Adversarial Attacks on embedded ConvNet models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18058)
*Mohsine EL Khayati,Abdelillah Semma,Abdelaziz Courr,Rachid Elouahbi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了阿拉伯语手写识别（AHR）深度学习模型在黑盒对抗攻击下的脆弱性。实验表明，Pixle攻击在多数模型上达到99-100%的攻击成功率，且生成的对抗样本在保持字符结构完整性的同时几乎不可被人类察觉，证明现有高性能AHR模型存在严重安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AHR研究过度关注识别性能，忽视了安全性问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索高性能AHR模型在真实世界黑盒攻击场景下的脆弱性。

Method: 在两种基准阿拉伯语手写字符数据集上，对多种深度学习模型实施黑盒对抗攻击实验。攻击者不具备模型架构的先验知识，模拟真实场景。主要攻击方法包括Pixle攻击及其他较温和的攻击策略。

Result: Pixle攻击在大多数模型上实现了99-100%的攻击成功率；其他攻击在大部分实验中达到50-96%的成功率。对抗样本在保持字符结构完整性的同时，对人眼几乎不可察觉。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前高性能AHR模型对对抗性操纵高度脆弱，亟需加强模型的安全防护研究，以确保其在现实应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Arabic handwriting recognition (AHR) has made significant progress with deep learning models. AHR research has largely focused on performance, with security receiving little attention. This study provides what appears to be a new line of inquiry by demonstrating the vulnerability of high-performing models to adversarial black-box attacks. The focus on black-box attacks reflects real-world scenarios where the attacker has no prior knowledge of the model architecture. Extensive experiments were conducted on two benchmark AHR datasets containing Arabic handwritten Characters. Results demonstrated the effectiveness of the attacks, with the Pixle attack achieving an attack success rate of 99-100\% on most models. Other, less aggressive attacks achieved success rates of 50-96\% across most experiments. Despite the higher attack success rate, the attacks maintain the structural integrity of the characters, rendering them almost imperceptible to the human eye. The findings indicate the higher vulnerability of the studied models to adversarial manipulation. This underscores the need to strengthen efforts to secure these models and ensure their reliability in AHR real-world applications.

</details>


### [188] [The MixCount Dataset: Bridging the Data Gap for Open-Vocabulary Object Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18063)
*Corentin Dumery,Niki Amini-Naieni,Shervin Naini,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixCount是一个用于混合目标计数的数据集和基准，通过自动生成管道合成带有像素级标注和细粒度文本描述的训练数据，解决了真实数据集标注成本高、噪声大以及现有合成数据多样性不足的问题。在该基准上，现有模型性能严重下降，但使用合成数据训练后，在真实基准FSC-147和PairTally上MAE分别降低20.14%和18.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前目标计数模型在混合目标场景（如工业检测、产品分拣）中系统性地失败，而真实计数数据集标注成本高昂且存在标签噪声，现有合成数据缺乏多样性和真实性，缺乏针对混合目标计数的专用数据集和基准。

Method: 开发了一个自动生成管道，能够大规模合成图像、细粒度文本描述和像素级完美计数标注，消除了先前数据集的标注歧义问题，构建了MixCount数据集和基准。

Result: 在MixCount基准上，现有最先进的计数模型性能严重下降；使用MixCount合成数据训练后，在FSC-147上MAE降低20.14%，在PairTally上MAE降低18.3%。

Conclusion: MixCount作为细粒度计数的基准和训练数据集，证明了自动合成管道能够生成几乎无限量的标注数据，有助于解决计数模型长期存在的瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Object counting is a foundational vision task with over a decade of dedicated research, yet state-of-the-art models still fail systematically in the mixed-object setting that dominates real-world applications such as industrial inspection and product sorting. We show that this gap is strongly driven by limitations in existing training and evaluation data: real counting datasets are prohibitively expensive to annotate and suffer from labeling noise, while existing synthetic alternatives lack diversity and realism. We address this with MixCount, a dataset and benchmark for mixed-object counting designed to target the failure modes of current counting models. To overcome the high cost of constructing and labeling such data, we develop an automatic generation pipeline that synthesizes images, fine-grained textual descriptions, and pixel-perfect counting annotations at scale, eliminating the labeling ambiguity that plagues prior datasets. Evaluating state-of-the-art counting models on MixCount exposes severe degradation in the mixed-object setting. More importantly, training these models on our synthesized data yields substantial gains on real-world benchmarks, reducing MAE by 20.14% on FSC-147 and by 18.3% on PairTally. These results establish MixCount as both a benchmark and a training dataset for fine-grained counting, and demonstrate that our pipeline, which produces effectively unlimited labeled data, helps address a long-standing bottleneck in counting models.

</details>


### [189] [SENSE: Satellite-based ENergy Synthesis for Sustainable Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18101)
*Kailai Sun,Mingyi He,Heye Huang,Can Rong,Alok Prakash,Baoshen Guo,Shenhao Wang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SENSE框架，一种基于可控扩散模型的生成式城市建筑能耗建模方法，通过卫星图像和路网等条件生成高质量的建筑能耗与高度图，仅需不到20%的标注数据即可提升下游预测性能10% IoU，并在四个城市验证了其物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有城市建筑能耗建模研究多为预测性方法，缺乏生成能力；生成式AI在卫星图像领域发展迅速但缺乏城市功能层（如能耗）的生成；高质量的配对能耗数据与卫星图像稀缺。

Method: 提出SENSE框架，基于可控扩散模型，以路网和城市密度指标为条件，利用大规模视觉模型在潜在空间中生成城市建筑能耗与高度图，实现卫星图像与能耗数据的联合生成。

Result: 在纽约、波士顿、里昂、釜山四个城市验证，SENSE实现了高视觉保真度和物理一致性（满足ASHRAE标准）；仅用不到20%的标注能耗数据即可生成足够的合成数据，下游预测性能提升10% IoU；与SOTA方法相比，预测误差降低3%-11% NMBE和1%-9% CVRMSE。

Conclusion: SENSE为城市科学、能源科学和建筑科学提供了一种节能导向的城市规划与物理生成解决方案，有助于实现联合国可持续发展目标7和11。

Abstract: Urban Building Energy Modeling plays a critical role in achieving the United Nations' Sustainable Development Goals 7 and 11. Although existing studies based on satellite imagery and deep learning have achieved remarkable progress, many challenges exist: most existing studies are inherently predictive, failing to reflect the generative nature of urban planning; although generative AI and diffusion models have seen explosive growth in satellite imagery, they lack the urban functional generation (e.g., energy layer); third, aligned high-quality high-resolution building energy data with satellite imagery is limited and scarce. Here we propose SENSE (Satellite-based ENergy Synthesis for Sustainable Environment), a unified generative UBEM framework that jointly synthesizes realistic urban satellite imagery and aligned high-quality building energy consumption and height maps. By conditioning on road networks and urban density metrics, SENSE, based on a controllable diffusion model, leverages the knowledge learned by large vision models to generate urban building energy consumption and height information (annotations) in the latent space. Experiments across four cities (New York City, Boston, Lyon, Busan) demonstrate that SENSE achieves high visual fidelity and strong physical consistency, satisfying the ASHRAE standard metric. Experiments demonstrate that SENSE can generate enough annotated synthetic data using less than 20% labeled energy data, boosting downstream prediction performance by 10% IoU. Compared to SOTA urban energy prediction methods, SENSE significantly reduced prediction error (reduced 3%-11% NMBE and 1%-9% CVRMSE). This study offers an energy-efficiency urban planning and physical generation solution for urban science, energy science and building science. The dataset and code: https://huggingface.co/datasets/skl24/MUSE and https://github.com/kailaisun/GenAI4Urban-Energy/.

</details>


### [190] [WinTok: A Win-Win Hybrid Tokenizer via Decomposing Visual Understanding and Generation with Transferable Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18115)
*Yiwei Guo,Shaobin Zhuang,Zhipeng Huang,Canmiao Fu,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WinTok是一种简洁的混合分词器，通过解耦语义token和像素token实现视觉理解与生成的统一，在不使用双分词器的情况下减少任务间干扰，并引入非对称token蒸馏机制增强语义能力，在10个基准测试中取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉分词器面临理解任务（高层语义抽象）与生成任务（底层像素重建）之间的固有冲突，单一token空间难以同时满足两种目标，亟需一种能有效解耦两类任务且不引入过大计算开销的统一方案。

Method: WinTok提出混合分词器架构：在像素token基础上补充一组可学习的语义token，显式解耦高层语义与底层像素重建目标；引入非对称token蒸馏机制，利用任意视觉基础模型的预训练语义嵌入引导语义token，使其继承强判别能力同时保持灵活性。

Result: 在10个挑战性基准测试中，WinTok在重建、理解和生成任务上均取得一致改进。仅使用5000万开源数据训练，在分类准确率上超越强基线UniTok 11.2%，重建rFID达到0.41。

Conclusion: WinTok通过解耦语义与像素token的方式有效缓解了视觉理解与生成之间的冲突，非对称token蒸馏进一步提升了理解能力，证明了简洁的混合设计可以在有限数据下取得优异性能。

Abstract: Building a unified visual tokenizer is essential for bridging the gap between visual understanding and generation. Yet existing approaches struggle with the inherent conflict between these tasks, as a single token space is forced to support both high-level semantic abstraction and low-level pixel reconstruction. We propose WinTok, a concise hybrid tokenizer that achieves a win-win performance by explicitly decoupling the two objectives. WinTok supplements pixel tokens with a set of learnable semantic tokens, effectively mitigating cross-task interference without incurring the computational overhead of dual tokenizers. To further enhance understanding capability, we introduce an asymmetric token distillation mechanism: the semantic tokens are guided by pretrained semantic embeddings from any visual foundation model, enabling them to inherit strong discriminative power while maintaining flexibility. Across 10 challenging benchmarks, WinTok delivers consistent improvements in reconstruction, understanding, and generation. Trained on only 50M open-source data, WinTok surpasses the strong baseline UniTok by 11.2% in classification accuracy and achieves a competitive reconstruction rFID of 0.41, despite using substantially less training data. Code is released at https://github.com/markywg/WinTok.

</details>


### [191] [Who Generated This 3D Asset? Learning Source Attribution for Generative 3D Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18132)
*Sihan Ma,Siyuan Liang,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了面向现代3D生成模型的被动源归属基准，覆盖22种生成器，并提出层次化多视图多模态Transformer，通过融合外观、几何和频域特征实现高精度溯源，在完整监督下达97.22%准确率，仅用1%训练数据仍达77.17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D生成模型在游戏、机器人、沉浸式创作中广泛应用，需要对生成的3D资产进行来源溯源，但面临两个核心挑战：归属信号分散于多视图、几何和频域线索中；现实部署中存在标签稀缺、提示退化、真伪混合等问题，导致归属可靠性下降。

Method: 构建了首个被动源归属基准，覆盖22种代表性3D生成器，包含标准、少样本和现实部署三种协议。提出层次化多视图多模态Transformer，在每个视图内融合外观、几何和频域特征，并建模跨视图全局关系，以捕捉生成模型留下的跨视图不一致性和结构伪影两类稳定指纹。

Result: 完整监督下准确率达97.22%，仅用1%训练数据（每生成器少于5个样本）仍达77.17%准确率，证明现代3D生成器留下稳定可归属的指纹。

Conclusion: 现代3D生成器存在稳定的可溯源指纹，本文建立的基准和方法为可信3D内容溯源提供了基础。

Abstract: Generative 3D models are deployed in gaming, robotics, and immersive creation, making source attribution critical: given a 3D asset, can we identify whether and which generative model created it? This problem faces two core challenges: dispersed attribution signals, where 3D fingerprints are distributed across multi-view, geometric, and frequency-domain cues; and realistic deployment constraints, where scarce labels, degraded prompts, and mixed real/synthetic assets undermine attribution reliability. To systematically study this problem, we construct, to the best of our knowledge, the first passive source attribution benchmark for modern generated assets, covering 22 representative 3D generators under standard, few-shot, and realistic deployment protocols. Based on this benchmark, we find that generative 3D models leave two types of stable fingerprints: cross-view inconsistency and structural artifacts reflected in geometric statistics and frequency-domain cues. To capture these dispersed signals, we propose a hierarchical multi-view multi-modal Transformer that fuses appearance, geometric, and frequency-domain features within each view and models global relationships across views. Extensive experiments demonstrate strong performance, achieving 97.22% accuracy under full supervision and 77.17% accuracy with only 1% training data, corresponding to fewer than five samples per generator. These results show that modern 3D generators leave stable and attributable fingerprints, establishing a new benchmark and methodological foundation for trustworthy 3D content provenance.

</details>


### [192] [Xiaomi EV World Model: A Joint World Model Integrating Reconstruction and Generation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18137)
*Lijun Zhou,Hongcheng Luo,Zhenxin Zhu,Cheng Chi,Mingfei Tu,Kaixin Xiong,Lei Gong,Zhanqian Wu,Zehan Zhang,Fangzhen Li,Hao Li,Yingying Shen,Jiale He,Haohui Zhu,Shan Zhao,Kai Wang,Zhiwei Zhan,Yuechuan Pu,Kaiyuan Tan,Ruiling Yang,Xianqi Wang,Tianyi Yan,Jiawei Zhou,Lei Zhang,Jingyang Zhao,Xi Zhou,Chitian Sun,Chenming Wu,Jiong Deng,Hongwei Xie,Ming Lu,Kun Ma,Long Chen,Guang Chen,Hangjun Ye,Bing Wang,Haiyang Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一套统一技术系统JWM，包含WorldRec（基于稀疏场景查询的前馈重建架构用于世界表示）和WorldGen（双向预训练+因果微调的三阶段渐进训练框架用于世界生成），两者深度融合实现了自动驾驶领域闭环仿真、数据合成与端到端训练所需的高质量世界建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶需要同时具备世界表示和世界生成两大核心能力，现有方法难以在统一框架下实现高保真的3D场景表征与高效的在线因果视频生成，且两者缺乏协同增益。

Method: 1) WorldRec：在3D空间初始化结构化查询，聚合跨视角、跨时间特征，构建紧凑高保真的3D高斯场景表示。2) WorldGen：两阶段训练框架（双向预训练+因果微调），因果微调包含三个渐进阶段（Teacher Forcing、ODE蒸馏、DMD），仅需4步去噪即可实现高质量在线因果视频生成。3) JWM：深度融合WorldRec和WorldGen，实现生成稳定性、跨帧一致性和视觉保真度的协同提升。

Result: JWM在生成稳定性、跨帧一致性和视觉保真度方面取得协同增益，仅需4步去噪即可实现高质量在线因果视频生成，为闭环仿真、数据合成和端到端训练提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: 通过WorldRec和WorldGen的深度集成，构建的统一世界模型JWM能够有效支撑自动驾驶中的闭环仿真、数据合成与端到端训练等关键应用。

Abstract: This report presents a unified technical system addressing the two core capabilities of world models for autonomous driving: world representation and world generation. For world representation, we propose WorldRec, a feed-forward reconstruction architecture driven by sparse scene queries. WorldRec initializes structured queries in 3D space, leveraging them to aggregate cross-view, cross-temporal features, thereby naturally enforcing spatial consistency across frames and yielding compact yet high-fidelity 3D Gaussian scene representations. For world generation, we propose WorldGen, a two-stage training framework of bidirectional pretraining followed by causal fine-tuning through three progressive stages (Teacher Forcing, ODE distillation, and DMD), enabling high-quality online causal video generation in as few as 4 denoising steps. Building on both modules, we further introduce the JWM, which deeply integrates WorldRec and WorldGen to achieve synergistic gains in generation stability, cross-frame consistency, and visual fidelity, providing a solid foundation for closed-loop simulation, data synthesis, and end-to-end training in autonomous driving.

</details>


### [193] [Semi-LAR: Semi-supervised Contrastive Learning with Linear Attention for Removal of Nighttime Flares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18156)
*Xiyu Zhu,Wei Wang,Kui Jiang,Zhengguo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种半监督镜头光晕去除框架，通过自适应伪标签库和光晕感知对比学习，在无标注图像上实现稳定学习，并显著提升性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光晕去除方法严重依赖大规模成对数据，而光晕伪影空间范围大且与场景结构纠缠，导致监督数据获取困难，亟需能从未标注图像中学习的半监督方法。

Method: 1) 自适应伪标签库：结合无参考质量评估、动量更新和无效标签过滤，渐进式优化伪监督信号，缓解误差累积；2) 光晕感知对比损失：将含光晕输入视为负样本，进行patch级对比学习，增强对光晕模式的判别性同时保持与可靠伪目标的一致性。

Result: 在多个光晕基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架具有模型无关性，能够持续提升去光晕的性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的半监督框架有效解决了光晕去除中伪标签不可靠和表示判别性不足的问题，为利用无标注数据提升去光晕效果提供了一种通用且稳定的方案。

Abstract: Lens flare removal is challenging due to the large spatial extent of flare artifacts and their entanglement with scene structures, while existing methods heavily rely on large-scale paired data. We propose a semi-supervised flare removal framework that enables stable learning from unlabeled images by jointly addressing pseudo-label reliability and representation discrimination. We propose an adaptive pseudo-label repository that progressively refines pseudo supervision through no-reference quality assessment, momentum-based updates, and invalid label filtering, effectively mitigating error accumulation. Moreover, we propose a flare-aware contrastive loss that explicitly treats flare-contaminated inputs as negatives and performs patch-level contrastive learning, encouraging representations that are discriminative against flare patterns while remaining consistent with reliable pseudo targets. Extensive experiments on multiple flare benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework is model-agnostic and consistently improves performance and robustness.

</details>


### [194] [Vision Inference Former: Sustaining Visual Consistency in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18160)
*Xinpeng Dong,Min Zhang,Kairong Han,Xu Tan,Fei Wu,Kun Kuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Vision Inference Former (VIF)，一种轻量级架构模块，通过在解码阶段持续注入视觉语义，解决多模态大语言模型中视觉信息被弱化以及长序列生成中视觉-语言对齐退化的问题。在14个基准任务上，VIF能在极小开销下持续提升多种架构的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs中，视觉信息被当作与文本token等同的处理，导致视觉模态的独特贡献被削弱；同时在长序列生成中，模型对视觉信息的依赖逐渐减弱，造成视觉-语言对齐退化。本文旨在解决这两个关键局限性。

Method: 提出Vision Inference Former (VIF)，一个轻量级架构模块，在推理的解码阶段持续将纯视觉表示直接注入模型的输出空间，建立视觉表征与输出之间的直接桥梁，确保生成过程始终以视觉内容为锚点。

Result: 在涵盖通用推理、OCR、表格理解、视觉评估和幻觉检测的14个基准任务上，VIF在多种不同架构上均能持续提升模型性能，且引入的额外计算开销极小。

Conclusion: VIF通过持续注入视觉语义，有效缓解了多模态大模型中视觉信息被弱化的问题，显著提升了视觉-语言对齐的鲁棒性，是一种通用且高效的改进方案。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress, primarily attributed to effective paradigms for integrating visual and textual information. The dominant connector-based paradigm projects visual features into textual sequence, enabling unified multimodal alignment and reasoning within a generative architecture. However, our experiments reveal two key limitations: (1) Although visual information serves as the core evidential modality in MLLMs, it is treated on par with textual tokens, diminishing the unique contribution of the visual modality; (2) As generation length increases, particularly within a limited context window, the model's dependence on visual information progressively weakens, resulting in deteriorated vision-language alignment and reduced consistency between generated content and visual semantics. To address these challenges, we propose the Vision Inference Former (VIF), a lightweight architectural module that establishes a direct bridge between pure visual representations and the model's output space. Specifically, VIF continuously injects visual semantics throughout the decoding phase of the inference process, ensuring that the model remains firmly grounded in visual content during generation. We conduct experiments on 14 benchmark tasks covering general reasoning, OCR, table understanding, vision-centric evaluation, and hallucination. Experimental results show that VIF consistently improves model performance across diverse architectures while introducing minimal additional overhead. The code for this work is available at https://github.com/Dong-Xinpeng/VIF.

</details>


### [195] [Self-Evolving Spatial Reasoning in Vision Language Models via Geometric Logic Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18162)
*Junming Liu,Yuqi Li,Yifei Sun,Maonan Wang,Piotr Koniusz,Yirong Chen,Ding Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAGE框架，通过几何与语言对偶变换的一致性约束，自进化地提升VLM空间推理的鲁棒性，在视频和空间推理基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在空间推理上存在脆弱性：模型能正确回答原始问题，但在可预测答案映射的成对变换下失效，说明实例级正确性与稳健空间推理之间存在差距。

Method: SAGE包含三部分：(1) 对偶一致性作为GRPO训练的辅助奖励，鼓励模型在原始输入和变换输入间产生逻辑一致的答案；(2) 动态操作池持续探测不一致性，促进挑战性操作、淘汰已掌握操作，使训练聚焦于最具信息量的信号；(3) 模型无关、数据高效，可作为轻量级后训练阶段应用于任何现有VLM。

Result: 在视频和空间推理基准上，SAGE相比强基线取得一致改进，并增强了向未见数据的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SAGE通过对偶一致性约束和动态操作池，有效弥补了VLM实例级正确性与稳健空间推理之间的鸿沟，是一种轻量、高效、可迁移的后训练方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have made striking progress, yet their spatial reasoning remains fragile: models that answer an original input correctly can still fail under paired transformations with predictable answer mappings, revealing a gap between instance-level correctness and robust spatial reasoning. To address this, we propose Spatial Alignment via Geometric Evolution (SAGE), a self-evolving framework that enforces logical consistency in VLMs through geometric and linguistic duality operations. SAGE incorporates duality consistency as an auxiliary reward within GRPO training, encouraging models to produce logically coherent answers across original and transformed inputs. A dynamic operation pool continuously probes for inconsistencies, promoting challenging operations and retiring mastered ones, so that training focuses on the most informative signals. SAGE is model-agnostic, data-efficient compared to prior GRPO methods, and can be applied as a lightweight post-training stage to any existing VLM. Experiments on video and spatial reasoning benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines and enhanced generalization to unseen data.

</details>


### [196] [Do You Need Text Rectification? Soft Attention Mask Embedding for Rectification-Free Scene Text Spotting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18173)
*Antonio Colombo,Giovanni Bianchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAME模块（软注意力掩膜嵌入模块），利用Transformer编码器的全局感受野编码高层特征并计算软注意力权重，与预测掩膜分层嵌入生成精细文本边界感知掩膜以抑制背景噪声；基于此构建SAME-Net端到端文本 spotting 框架，无需字符级标注或辅助文本校正模块。在Total-Text数据集上达到84.02%端到端H均值，超越GLASS 1.02%；在ICDAR 2015上获得83.4%强词典结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文本 spotting 方法因多尺度变化、任意文本形状及复杂背景干扰导致掩膜预测不完整，影响识别精度。

Method: 提出软注意力掩膜嵌入模块（SAME），利用Transformer编码器的全局感受野编码高层特征并计算软注意力权重，将其与预测掩膜分层嵌入生成文本边界感知掩膜；SAME模块完全可微，允许识别损失梯度反向传播至检测分支，实现检测与识别的联合优化。

Result: 在Total-Text上达到84.02%端到端H均值，超越GLASS 1.02%；在ICDAR 2015上获得83.4%强词典结果。

Conclusion: SAME-Net通过软注意力机制有效抑制背景噪声，无需字符级标注或辅助校正模块，在多个基准上达到SOTA。

Abstract: End-to-end scene text spotting, which unifies text detection and recognition within a single framework, has witnessed remarkable progress driven by deep learning advances. However, most existing approaches still suffer from incomplete mask proposals caused by multi-scale variation, arbitrary text shapes, and complex background interference, thereby degrading recognition accuracy. In this paper, we propose a novel Soft Attention Mask Embedding module (SAME) that leverages the global receptive field of Transformer encoders to encode high-level features and compute soft attention weights, which are then hierarchically embedded with predicted masks to generate refined text-boundary-aware masks that effectively suppress background noise. Building upon this module, we present SAME-Net, a robust end-to-end text spotting framework that requires neither character-level annotations nor auxiliary text rectification modules. Since the soft attention mechanism is fully differentiable, recognition loss gradients can be back-propagated through the SAME module to the detection branch, enabling joint optimization of detection and recognition objectives. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach: SAME-Net achieves 84.02\% end-to-end H-mean on the arbitrarily-shaped Total-Text dataset, surpassing the previous state-of-the-art GLASS by 1.02\% in full-lexicon accuracy without additional training data, and obtains competitive 83.4\% strong-lexicon results on the multi-oriented ICDAR 2015 dataset.

</details>


### [197] [MARS: Technical Report for the CASTLE Challenge at EgoVis 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18176)
*Haoyu Zhang,Qiaohui Chu,Yisen Feng,Meng Liu,Weili Guan,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MARS是一个多模态智能体推理系统，通过选择性检索视频、转录文本及辅助模态（凝视、心率、照片、热成像）的证据，在CASTLE Challenge中回答跨四天活动、15个同步视角的185个封闭式问题，最终获得第二名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多为单视频第一人称视角，而CASTLE要求推理跨四天、15个同步视角及多种辅助模态的数据，传统纯文本流水线无法处理如此复杂的多模态证据选择问题。

Method: 首先按官方目录组织构建证据记忆库（视频+转录文本为主，凝视/心率/照片/热成像为辅）；长视频压缩为字幕和DeepSeek摘要；推理时GPT-5.4决策智能体反复选择：继续推理、请求缺失模态、生成答案或随机回退。

Result: 在CASTLE Challenge最终排行榜上获得第二名。

Conclusion: 将复杂多模态问答任务建模为智能体证据选择问题，有效整合异构多模态源，验证了智能体式推理优于纯文本流水线。

Abstract: This report presents MARS, short for Multimodal Agentic Reasoning with Source selection, our system for the CASTLE Challenge at EgoVis 2026. Participants must answer 185 closed-form questions over the CASTLE 2024 dataset. In contrast to prior single-video egocentric benchmarks, CASTLE requires reasoning over four days of activity, 15 synchronized perspectives, official transcripts, and multiple auxiliary modalities, including personal photos, auxiliary videos, gaze, thermal imagery, and heartrate measurements. MARS therefore treats the task as an agentic evidence-selection problem over multimodal sources rather than a purely text-only pipeline. MARS first follows the official CASTLE directory organization to build evidence memories from two primary sources, videos and transcripts, and four auxiliary sources, gaze, heartrate, photos, and thermal imagery. Long videos are converted into captions and DeepSeek-based summaries only because CASTLE videos are too long to fit directly into the model context for every question; this step compresses temporal evidence while keeping photos and other auxiliary media available as source-specific evidence. At inference time, a GPT-5.4 decision agent repeatedly chooses whether to continue reasoning, request a specific missing modality, produce an answer, or fall back to a random option when the evidence remains insufficient. The resulting system achieved second place on the final CASTLE Challenge leaderboard. Our codes are available at https://github.com/Hyu-Zhang/MARS.

</details>


### [198] [View-Aware Semantic Alignment for Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18192)
*Quan Zhang,Zeqiang Cai,Peiming Zhao,Jingze Wu,Cailun Wu,Hongbo Chen,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViSA（视图感知语义对齐）框架，通过专家驱动的令牌生成模块和双分支局部融合模块，实现无人机与固定相机之间的人物重识别，解决了视角剧烈变化带来的挑战，在三个基准数据集上取得最优性能，尤其在CARGO跨视角协议上mAP提升10.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AGPReID方法采用视角不变范式进行部件级对齐，忽略了视角特有线索和判别性身份信息，导致跨视角人物重识别性能受限。

Method: 提出ViSA框架，包含两个核心模块：1）专家驱动令牌生成模块（ETGM），构建一组视角感知专家以生成自适应语义查询，捕捉视角特定模式；2）双分支局部融合模块（DLFM），利用图推理提取并对齐对不同专家响应的局部区域。

Result: 在AG-ReID.v2、CARGO和LAGPeR三个AGPReID基准数据集上取得最优性能，在CARGO跨视角协议上mAP提升10.06%。

Conclusion: ViSA通过视图感知语义对齐范式替代传统的视角不变范式，有效提升了跨视角人物重识别的性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Aerial-Ground Person Re-Identification (AGPReID) remains highly challenging due to drastic viewpoint variations between drones and fixed cameras. Existing methods typically follow a view-invariant paradigm, aligning shared features across views to achieve robustness. However, view-invariant inherently enforces part-level alignment, which ignores view-specific cues and discriminative identity information. To this end, this work proposes ViSA (View-aware Semantic Alignment), a view-aware framework that achieves cross-view semantic consistency containing an Expert-driven Token Generation Module (ETGM) and a Dual-branch Local Fusion Module (DLFM). Technically, the former constructs a set of view-aware experts to generate adaptive semantic queries that perceive viewpoint-specific patterns, while the latter leverages graph reasoning to extract and align local regions responsive to different experts. Extensive experiments on three AGPReID benchmarks including AG-ReID.v2, CARGO and LAGPeR demonstrate that ViSA consistently achieves superior performance, with a notable 10.06\% mAP improvement on the challenging CARGO cross-view protocol. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/Cat-Zero/ViSA}{https://github.com/Cat-Zero/ViSA}.

</details>


### [199] [Best Segmentation Buddies for Image-Shape Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18193)
*Itai Lang,Dongwei Lyu,Dale Decatur,Rana Hanocka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Best Segmentation Buddies (BSB)的方法，用于在图像与未纹理3D形状之间建立分割到分割的语义对应。该方法通过将2D视觉模型的特征蒸馏到3D表面，利用特征相似性找到“最佳分割伙伴”顶点，并借助2D图像分割模型对3D形状进行直接分割，实现了跨模态、跨视角的鲁棒语义对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像与未纹理3D形状之间因外观、几何和视角的巨大差异，导致跨模态分割对应任务极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效解决这一被忽视的问题。

Method: 首先将2D视觉模型的深层特征蒸馏到3D形状表面，计算图像像素与形状顶点间的特征相似度；然后识别“最佳分割伙伴”（BSB）——那些最相似图像像素位于图像分割区域内的顶点；最后利用从2D图像分割模型蒸馏的3D特征直接在3D中分割形状，引导对应过程。

Result: 在多种图像-形状对上的实验表明，该方法能够生成准确且语义上有意义的对应关系，验证了其通用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过跨模态特征蒸馏和最佳分割伙伴机制，有效弥合了图像与未纹理3D形状之间的语义鸿沟，为无纹理3D形状与图像之间的语义对应提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Finding correspondences is a fundamental and extensively researched problem in computer vision and graphics. In this work, we examine the underexplored task of estimating segmentation-to-segmentation correspondence between images in the wild and untextured 3D shapes. This task is highly challenging due to substantial differences in appearance, geometry, and viewpoint. Our approach bridges the cross-modality gap by linking pixels in the image segment to vertices in the corresponding semantic part of the 3D shape. To achieve this, we first distill deep visual features from a 2D vision model onto the 3D shape surface, allowing for the computation of feature similarity between image pixels and shape vertices. Then, we identify Best Segmentation Buddies, vertices whose most similar image pixel lies within the image segmentation region, enabling the reliable discovery of vertices in semantically corresponding shape parts. Finally, we leverage distilled 3D features from the 2D image segmentation model to segment the shape directly in 3D, bootstrapping the correspondence process. We demonstrate the generality and robustness of our approach across a wide range of image-shape pairs, showcasing accurate and semantically meaningful correspondences. Our project page is at https://threedle.github.io/bsb/.

</details>


### [200] [EgoInteract: Synthetic Egocentric Videos Generation for Interaction Understanding and Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18214)
*Rosario Leonardi,Francesco Ragusa,Daniele Materia,Alessandro Passanisi,James Fort,Jakob Engel,Giovanni Maria Farinella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoInteract，一个可控的自我中心视频生成模拟器，用于建模细粒度的人-物交互及时间动态。基于该框架生成的合成数据集在多个真实自我中心视频基准测试中，对时间动作分割、下一活动目标检测、交互预测和手-物交互检测等任务均取得了优于强基线的性能，验证了模拟方法的有效性和可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模自我中心视频数据集在采集时空密集标注时成本高、速度慢，且受限于环境偏差、隐私约束和交互模式覆盖不足，因此需要探索合成数据在自我中心感知任务中的潜力。

Method: 提出了EgoInteract可控模拟器，实现对相机、人体/手部运动、物体操作和场景构成的精确控制，并基于此生成带有密集时空标注的合成自我中心视频数据集。

Result: 在多个真实世界自我中心视频基准上，使用模拟数据训练的模型在时间动作分割、下一活动目标检测、交互预测和手-物交互检测任务中均取得了相较于强基线的一致改进。

Conclusion: 基于模拟的自我中心视频生成方法在多个下游任务上有效且具有良好的跨环境、跨物体类别和跨交互模式的迁移能力。

Abstract: Collecting large-scale egocentric video datasets with dense spatial and temporal annotations is costly, slow, and often constrained by environmental biases, privacy constraints, and limited coverage of interaction patterns. While synthetic data has shown strong potential in several vision domains, its use for egocentric perception remains relatively underexplored, especially for tasks requiring temporally coherent human-object interactions. In this work, we introduce EgoInteract, a controllable simulator for egocentric video generation designed to model fine-grained egocentric interactions and their temporal dynamics. The simulator enables precise control over camera, human body and hand motion, object manipulation, and scene composition across diverse environments. Building on this framework, we generate a synthetic egocentric video dataset with dense spatial and temporal annotations for temporal action segmentation, next-active object detection, interaction anticipation, and hand-object interaction detection. We evaluate models trained with simulated data on multiple real-world egocentric benchmarks spanning diverse environments, object categories, and interaction patterns. Results show consistent improvements over strong baselines across tasks and datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness and transferability of our simulation-based approach.

</details>


### [201] [Enhancing Train-Free Infinite-Frame Generation for Consistent Long Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18233)
*X. Feng,J. Zhu,M. Wu,C. Chen,F. Mao,H. Guo,J. Wu,X. Chu,K. Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIGA是一种无需额外训练即可生成长视频的方法，通过两阶段对齐机制减少训练-推理差距，并利用自反思与长程帧引导双重一致性增强机制提升时间一致性，在VBench和NarrLV上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 免训练长视频生成虽能利用基础视频生成模型生成更长视频，但存在训练与推理不匹配以及长期一致性难以维持的问题，限制了基础模型的有效利用。

Method: 提出MIGA方法：1）两阶段对齐机制，通过减少送入模型的过度噪声跨度来缓解训练-推理差距；2）双重一致性增强机制，包括自反思方法修正早期高噪声帧，以及长程帧引导方法利用后期低噪声帧的广覆盖来引导生成，共同提升时间一致性。

Result: 在VBench和NarrLV基准上的大量实验表明MIGA达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: MIGA通过两阶段对齐和双重一致性增强，有效解决了免训练长视频生成中的训练-推理差距和长期一致性问题，无需额外计算开销即可实现无限帧长视频生成。

Abstract: Without incurring significant computational overhead, train-free long video generation aims to enable foundation video generation models to produce longer videos. Frame-level autoregressive frameworks, e.g., FIFO-diffusion, offer the advantage of generating infinitely long videos with constant memory consumption. However, the mismatch between training and inference, coupled with the challenge of maintaining long-term consistency, limits the effective utilization of foundation models. To mitigate these concerns, we propose \textbf{MIGA}, a novel infinite-frame long video generation method. Firstly, we propose an effective two-stage alignment mechanism that mitigates the training-inference gap by reducing the excessive noise span fed to the model. We then introduce an innovative dual consistency enhancement mechanism, where the self-reflection approach corrects early high-noise frames and the long-range frame guidance approach leverages later low-noise frames with broad coverage to steer generation, jointly improving temporal consistency. Extensive experiments on VBench and NarrLV demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of MIGA. Our project page is available at https://xiaokunfeng.github.io/miga_homepage/.

</details>


### [202] [GaussianZoom: Progressive Zoom-in Generative 3D Gaussian Splatting with Geometric and Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18252)
*Jiale Shi,Jiarui Hu,Zesong Yang,Kaixuan Luan,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GaussianZoom提出了一个迭代渐进式框架，结合几何一致性场景建模和多尺度语义推理，实现从低分辨率输入到高保真极端放大渲染的生成式3D重建系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在极端放大渲染时面临分辨率不足、多视点不一致和跨尺度伪影等问题，缺乏有效的生成式放大机制。

Method: 1) 多视点一致超分辨率模块：基于深度特征变形和VLM驱动的细节合成；2) 可扩展连续LOD层次结构：动态调制高斯可见性以实现平滑、无混叠的跨尺度渲染。

Result: 在Mip-NeRF360和Tanks&Temples数据集上，GaussianZoom在极端放大条件下取得了优越的感知质量、多视点一致性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: GaussianZoom为生成式放大3D场景重建建立了强基线，证明结合几何一致性建模与多尺度语义推理可以有效解决极端放大渲染中的关键挑战。

Abstract: We introduce GaussianZoom, a generative zoom-in 3D reconstruction system with an iterative progressive framework that combines geometry-consistent scene modeling and multi-scale semantic reasoning to enable high-fidelity extreme zoom-in rendering from low-resolution inputs. To achieve this, we develop a novel multi-view consistent super-resolution module with depth-based feature warping and VLM-driven detail synthesis, ensuring accurate multi-view correspondence while enriching fine-scale appearance beyond the observed resolution. To support zooming across large magnification ranges, we further introduce a new expandable continuous Level-of-Detail hierarchy that dynamically modulates Gaussian visibility for smooth, alias-free cross-scale rendering. Experiments on Mip-NeRF360 and Tanks\&Temples demonstrate that GaussianZoom achieves superior perceptual quality, multi-view consistency, and robustness under extreme magnification, establishing a strong baseline for generative zoom-in 3D scene reconstruction.

</details>


### [203] [CodeBind: Decoupled Representation Learning for Multimodal Alignment with Unified Compositional Codebook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18257)
*Zeyu Chen,Jie Li,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeBind提出了一种通过模态共享-特定码本设计优化多模态表示空间的框架，采用组合向量量化方案，将特征分解为共享组件和特定组件，在九种模态上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态表示对齐方法受限于跨模态信息差异和数据稀缺性，导致对齐空间次优且忽略了模态特有特征。

Method: 提出CodeBind框架，采用模态共享-特定码本设计，通过组合向量量化方案将特征分解为共享组件（保持语义一致性）和特定组件（保留模态独特细节），并利用增量对齐目标模态和桥接模态，无需完全配对数据。

Result: 在文本、图像、视频、音频、深度、热成像、触觉、3D点云、脑电图九种模态上，多模态分类和检索任务达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: CodeBind通过共享码本桥接模态间隙、特定码本缓解表示偏差，有效解决了多模态对齐中的信息差异和数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Multimodal representation alignment is pivotal for large language models and robotics. Traditional methods are often hindered by cross-modal information discrepancies and data scarcity, leading to suboptimal alignment spaces that overlook modality-unique features. We propose CodeBind, a framework that optimizes multimodal representation spaces through a modality-shared-specific codebook design. By incrementally aligning target and bridging modalities, CodeBind bypasses the need for fully paired data. Unlike traditional hard alignment, CodeBind decomposes features into shared components for semantic consistency and specific components for modality-unique details. This design utilizes a compositional vector quantization scheme, where a shared codebook bridges modality gaps and modality-specific codebooks mitigate representation bias by preventing dominant modalities from overshadowing others. Validated across nine modalities (text, image, video, audio, depth, thermal, tactile, 3D point cloud, EEG), CodeBind achieves state-of-the-art performance in multimodal classification and retrieval tasks.

</details>


### [204] [RT-Splatting: Joint Reflection-Transmission Modeling with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18263)
*Ji Shi,Xianghua Ying,Bowei Xing,Ruohao Guo,Wenzhen Yue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RT-Splatting通过解耦几何占据与光学不透明度，实现了对半透明镜面场景的高质量实时新视角合成，在反射与透射渲染上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS方法在处理半透明镜面表面时难以同时兼顾复杂反射和清晰透射，常产生模糊反射或过度遮挡的透射效果。

Method: 1) 解耦每个高斯原语的几何占据与光学不透明度，形成统一的表面-体积场景表示；2) 设计混合渲染器，同时以表面方式捕捉高频反射、以体积方式保留清晰透射；3) 提出Specular-Aware Gradient Gating，抑制高镜面区域对透射分支的误导梯度，减少漂浮物。

Result: 在挑战性半透明场景上达到SOTA性能，实现高保真反射与清晰透射的实时渲染，并支持灵活的场景编辑。

Conclusion: RT-Splatting通过几何-光学解耦有效解决了半透明镜面场景的渲染难题，兼顾实时性与高质量，并为场景编辑提供了自然支持。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis with high visual quality. However, existing methods struggle with semi-transparent specular surfaces that exhibit both complex reflections and clear transmission, often producing blurry reflections or overly occluded transmission. To address this, we present RT-Splatting, a framework that disentangles each Gaussian's geometric occupancy from its optical opacity. This factorization yields a unified surface-volume scene representation with a single set of Gaussian primitives. Our hybrid renderer interprets this representation both as a surface to capture high-frequency reflections and as a volume to preserve clear transmission. To mitigate the ambiguity in jointly optimizing reflection and transmission, we introduce Specular-Aware Gradient Gating, which suppresses misleading gradients from highly specular regions into the transmission branch, effectively reducing distracting floaters. Experiments on challenging semi-transparent scenes show that RT-Splatting achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering high-fidelity reflections and clear transmission with real-time rendering. Moreover, our factorization naturally enables flexible scene editing. The project page is available at https://sjj118.github.io/RT-Splatting.

</details>


### [205] [SRC-Flow: Compact Semantic Representations Enable Normalizing Flows for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18267)
*Longtao Jiang,Jiangmin Bao,Zhendong Wang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Zhihui Li,Xiaojun Chang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SRC-Flow通过引入语义表示压缩器（SRC）将高维RAE特征压缩到低维语义空间再进行流建模，解决了归一化流在高维表示空间中建模负担过重的问题，在ImageNet 256×256和512×512上实现了归一化流方法中最优的生成质量（gFID分别为1.65和2.07），同时保留了精确似然计算和确定性可逆采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流在图像生成任务上长期落后于扩散模型，根本原因是NFs需要在全环境空间学习单一的可逆传输，对高维表示极为敏感，导致现代视觉表示空间中存在语义-容量不匹配问题：语义信息本身紧凑但被编码在过完备特征中。

Method: 提出SRC-Flow，核心组件为语义表示压缩器（SRC），在流建模之前将高维RAE特征压缩到低维语义空间，并通过冻结的RAE解码器保留重建能力；同时采用针对固定无条件双射的常数噪声正则化策略。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256和512×512上，SRC-Flow在无分类器引导下gFID分别达到1.65和2.07，为所有归一化流方法中的最优结果。

Conclusion: SRC-Flow通过语义空间压缩有效缓解了NFs与高维表示之间的语义-容量不匹配问题，在保持精确似然计算和确定性可逆采样的同时，显著提升了归一化流的大规模图像生成能力。

Abstract: Normalizing flows (NFs) provide exact likelihoods and deterministic invertible sampling, but have historically lagged behind diffusion models for large-scale image generation. We identify a key obstacle: NFs are required to learn a single invertible transport over the full ambient space, making them highly sensitive to high-dimensional representations. This leads to a semantic-capacity mismatch in modern visual representation spaces, where semantic information is compact but encoded in overcomplete features. We propose SRC-Flow, which introduces a Semantic Representation Compressor (SRC) to compact high-dimensional RAE features into a low-dimensional semantic space before flow modeling and preserve reconstruction through the frozen RAE decoder. This compact space reduces the modeling burden of NFs and enables effective likelihood-based generation in semantic representation space. We further adopt constant noise regularization tailored to the fixed unconditional bijection learned by flows. On ImageNet $256 \times 256$ and $512 \times 512$, SRC-Flow achieves state-of-the-art generation quality among normalizing flow methods, with gFID scores of 1.65 and 2.07 under classifier-free guidance, while retaining exact likelihood computation in the compact semantic representation space and deterministic invertible sampling at the flow level. Codes and models will be available at https://github.com/longtaojiang/SRC-Flow.

</details>


### [206] [StableVLA: Towards Robust Vision-Language-Action Models without Extra Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18287)
*Yiyang Fu,Chubin Zhang,Shukai Gong,Yufan Deng,Kaiwei Sun,Qiyang Min,Qibin Hou,Yansong Tang,Jianan Wang,Daquan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出信息瓶颈适配器（IB-Adapter），一种轻量级模块（<10M参数），通过信息论原理选择性过滤视觉扰动，使VLA模型在未见过干扰下平均提升30%性能，且0.5B参数的StableVLA即可达到7B模型级别的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据无法涵盖所有可能的视觉干扰，导致VLA模型在遇到未见过的真实世界视觉扰动时性能显著下降，亟需高效的鲁棒性增强方案。

Method: 提出基于信息瓶颈理论的轻量级适配器模块IB-Adapter，从信息论角度选择性过滤视觉输入中的潜在噪声，无需额外数据或数据增强策略。

Result: IB-Adapter以<10M参数增量使基线模型平均提升30%；基于0.5B参数骨干的StableVLA无需Open X-Embodiment预训练，鲁棒性即可媲美7B规模SOTA模型；在长程任务和物理/合成视觉损坏下均优于OpenPi。

Conclusion: IB-Adapter以极小参数开销显著提升VLA模型对未知视觉扰动的鲁棒性，验证了信息瓶颈方法在视觉-语言-动作建模中的有效性。

Abstract: It is infeasible to encompass all possible disturbances within the training dataset. This raises a critical question regarding the robustness of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models when encountering unseen real-world visual disturbances, particularly under imperfect visual conditions. In this work, we conduct a systematic study based on recent state-of-the-art VLA models and reveal a significant performance drop when visual disturbances absent from the training data are introduced. To mitigate this issue, we propose a lightweight adapter module grounded in information theory, termed the Information Bottleneck Adapter (IB-Adapter), which selectively filters potential noise from visual inputs. Without requiring any extra data or augmentation strategies, IB-Adapter consistently improves over the baseline by an average of 30%, while adding fewer than 10M parameters, demonstrating notable efficiency and effectiveness. Furthermore, even with a 14x smaller backbone (0.5B parameters) and no pre-training on the Open X-Embodiment dataset, our model StableVLA achieves robustness competitive with 7B-scale state-of-the-art VLAs. With negligible parameter overhead (<10M), our approach maintains accuracy on long-horizon tasks and surpasses OpenPi under both synthetic and physical visual corruptions.

</details>


### [207] [Collision-Resistant Single-Pass Method for Unsupervised Fine-Grained Image Hashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18288)
*Anh-Kiet Duong,Petra Gomez-Krämer,Jean-Michel Carozza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CS3H框架，通过单遍归一化汉明距离损失和碰撞敏感注意力模块，实现无监督细粒度图像哈希中的抗碰撞二进制编码，在多个基准上取得最优检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督细粒度图像哈希方法忽略了碰撞抵抗问题，导致语义略有差异的样本被映射到相同的哈希码，从而降低了检索的区分度。

Method: 提出CS3H框架：1) 单遍归一化汉明距离损失直接优化汉明空间相似度；2) 碰撞敏感注意力模块强调稀有且具判别性的局部模式，减少哈希碰撞。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，CS3H在检索精度上持续超越现有最先进方法，同时以极小的计算开销实现了优异的抗碰撞性能。

Conclusion: CS3H通过直接优化汉明空间相似度并引入碰撞敏感注意力，有效解决了无监督细粒度哈希中的碰撞问题，在不增加显著计算负担的前提下提升了检索区分度。

Abstract: Unsupervised fine-grained image hashing aims to learn compact binary codes that preserve subtle visual differences among highly similar instances without manual annotations. However, most existing methods neglect collision resistance, leading to identical hash codes for slightly semantically different samples. In this paper, we propose Collision-Resistant Single-Pass Self-Supervised Semantic Hashing (CS3H), a collision-resistant framework that directly optimizes Hamming-space similarity via a single-pass normalized Hamming distance loss to produce well-separated binary representations. We further introduce a collision-sensitive attention module to emphasize rare and discriminative local patterns, reducing hash collisions and improving fine-grained discrimination. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that CS3H consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in retrieval accuracy while achieving superior collision resistance with minimal computational overhead.

</details>


### [208] [Wasserstein Equilibrium Decoding for Reliable Medical Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18313)
*Luca Hagen,Johanna P. Müller,Weitong Zhang,Mengyun Qiao,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对小型视觉语言模型（2-8B）在临床部署中容易产生看似合理但错误输出的问题，将博弈论解码扩展至开放域医学视觉问答任务，并提出基于语义感知的Wasserstein停止准则替代词汇级顺序匹配，从而实现语义共识上的收敛并避免不必要的迭代。在VQA-RAD和PathVQA上取得一致且统计显著的改进，同时将平均收敛迭代次数减少约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型视觉语言模型（2-8B）因隐私、连接性和低延迟需求适合临床部署，但其有限容量加剧了产生看似合理但错误输出的问题（幻觉）。现有博弈论解码方法仅限于文本型、封闭式NLP任务，无法直接应用于开放式的医学视觉问答。

Method: 将博弈论解码（BDG）从文本封闭式任务扩展到视觉语言模型的开放域医学VQA；提出语义感知的Wasserstein停止准则，基于候选答案之间的语义共识（而非词汇级顺序匹配）判断收敛，避免因临床等效但排名互换导致的不必要迭代。

Result: 在VQA-RAD上，Qwen3-VL-2B提升+3.5个百分点（p<0.01），超越贪心解码的4B模型；在PathVQA上，Gemma-3-4B+BDG无需领域微调即可匹配MedGemma-4B贪心解码性能；Wasserstein准则在保持精度不变下将平均收敛迭代减少约20%。

Conclusion: 语义感知的Wasserstein停止准则能有效提升小型VLM在医学VQA上的准确性与推理效率，博弈论解码方法的扩展适用于多模态开放域任务。

Abstract: Small vision-language models (2-8B) are well-suited for clin- ical deployment due to privacy constraints, limited connectivity, and low-latency requirements favouring on-device or on-premise inference. However, their limited capacity exacerbates the generation of plausible but incorrect outputs. We extend game-theoretic decoding, previously restricted to text-only, closed-ended NLP tasks, to vision-language mod- els for open-ended Medical VQA. We introduce a semantically aware Wasserstein stopping criterion that replaces lexical order matching, en- abling convergence based on semantic consensus among near-synonymous candidate answers and avoiding unnecessary iterations caused by clini- cally equivalent ranking swaps. On VQA-RAD and PathVQA, we ob- tain consistent, statistically significant improvements over greedy and discriminative baselines. On VQA-RAD, we improve Qwen3-VL-2B by +3.5 percentage points (p < 0.01), surpassing the greedy 4B model, with similar trends at larger scales. On PathVQA, Gemma-3-4B with BDG matches MedGemma-4B under greedy decoding despite no domain- specific fine-tuning. At accuracy parity with classic BDG, the Wasser- stein criterion reduces average convergence iterations by approximately 20%, improving inference efficiency while preserving the game-theoretic equilibrium behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/luca-hagen/ Wasserstein-BDG-medical-VQA.

</details>


### [209] [Improved Baselines with Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18324)
*Jaskirat Singh,Boyang Zheng,Zongze Wu,Richard Zhang,Eli Shechtman,Saining Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEv2，通过三项关键改进：1）使用最后k层编码器层的和作为表征（而非仅最后一层）；2）发现RAE与REPA具有互补机制；3）利用REPA实现无成本的无分类器指导（CFG），使训练速度提升10倍以上，在ImageNet-256上仅80个epoch即达到SOTA gFID 1.06，并引入EP_FID@k作为训练效率度量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始RAE存在三大问题：重建质量依赖编码器微调或专用数据；RAE与REPA的关系不明确；RAE在无分类器指导（CFG）上表现不佳，需要额外训练一个较弱的扩散模型进行AutoGuidance（AG）。本文旨在系统性地研究并解决这些问题，简化并改进RAE。

Method: 1）将表征定义为最后k层编码器层的和，而非仅最后一层；2）通过大规模实证分析揭示RAE与REPA的互补工作机制；3）将REPA视为RAE潜空间中的x-prediction，通过重新参数化DiT模型的输出，实现"免费"的CFG指导。

Result: 在ImageNet-256上仅80个epoch即达到gFID 1.06（SOTA）；在FDr^k上80个epoch达到2.17，远超此前最佳3.26（800个epoch）；EP_FID@2仅为35个epoch（原始RAE为177）；在文本到图像生成和导航世界模型等多样化场景中均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: RAEv2通过三项简化的设计改进，实现了比原始RAE快10倍以上的收敛速度，并在多个任务上取得SOTA结果。同时提出了EP_FID@k作为衡量训练效率的新指标。

Abstract: Representation Autoencoders (RAE) replace traditional VAE with pretrained vision encoders. In this paper, we systematically investigate several design choices and find three insights which simplify and improve RAE. First, we study a generalized formulation where the representation is defined as sum of the last k encoder layers rather than solely the final layer. This simple change greatly improves reconstruction without encoder finetuning or specialized data (e.g., text, faces). Second, we study the prevalent assumption that RAE (using pretrained representation as encoder) replaces representation alignment (REPA), which distills the same representation to intermediate layers instead. Through large-scale empirical analysis, we uncover a surprising finding: RAE and REPA exhibit complementary working mechanisms, allowing the same representation to be used as both encoder and target for intermediate diffusion layers. Finally, the original RAE struggles with classifier-free guidance (CFG) and requires training a second, weaker diffusion model for AutoGuidance (AG). We show that REPA itself can be viewed as x-prediction in RAE latent space. By simply re-parameterizing the output of the DiT model, it can provide guidance for "free". Overall, RAEv2 leads to more than 10x faster convergence over the original RAE, achieving a state-of-the-art gFID of 1.06 in just 80 epochs on ImageNet-256. On FDr^k, RAEv2 achieves a state-of-the-art 2.17 at just 80 epochs compared to the previous best 3.26 (800 epochs) without any post-training. This motivates EP_FID@k (epochs to reach unguided gFID <= k) as a measure of training efficiency. RAEv2 attains an EP_FID@2 of 35 epochs, versus 177 for the original RAE. We also validate our approach across diverse settings for text-to-image generation and navigation world models, showing consistent improvements. Code is available at https://raev2.github.io.

</details>


### [210] [CineMatte: Background Matting for Virtual Production and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18328)
*Yuanjian He,Chen Zhang,Fasheng Chen,Jiangbo Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CineMatte 是一种针对LED虚拟制片场景的鲁棒背景抠图框架，采用交叉注意力条件设计的Siamese DINOv3架构，并用预训练图像引导上采样器替代传统细节分支，同时发布了首个4K HDR虚拟制片抠图数据集CineMatte-4K，在VP和公开基准上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LED虚拟制片（VP）虽能实时渲染背景实现机内视觉特效，但拍摄完成后修改背景极为耗时费力，现有抠图方法在VP场景下对背景偏移鲁棒性不足，且缺乏专门的非合成VP抠图数据集。

Method: 采用Siamese双流架构，共享权重的冻结DINOv3 Vision Transformer分别编码输入帧和背景图，通过交叉注意力模块比较两流预测前景；用预训练图像引导特征上采样器替代传统并行卷积细节分支以消除边界伪影；构建包含图像和视频子集的CineMatte-4K数据集（图像通过绿幕插入获得非合成真值，视频包含带轨迹追踪的相机运动）。

Result: 在自建的CineMatte-4K以及VideoMatte240K、YouTubeMatte等公开基准上，CineMatte不仅在虚拟制片场景中表现优异，且能鲁棒地泛化到真实世界 footage。

Conclusion: CineMatte通过交叉注意力条件设计和预训练上采样器有效解决了VP场景下的背景抠图问题，其非合成数据集也为该领域提供了重要基准。

Abstract: LED Virtual Production (VP) uses large LED volumes to render backgrounds in real time, enabling in-camera visual effects but making post-shot changes labor-intensive. We address this with CineMatte, a robust background matting framework for VP and beyond. CineMatte employs a cross-attention-conditioned design. Instead of concatenating the background with the input, CineMatte employs a Siamese, frozen DINOv3 Vision Transformer with shared weights to encode the input frame and the captured background separately. A cross-attention module compares the two streams to predict the foreground, preserving pretrained semantics and improving robustness to background shifts. Previous ViT-based matting models use a parallel convolutional "detail branch" to recover fine details, which can cause boundary artifacts in real-world samples due to semantic misalignment with the backbone. We instead replace it with a pretrained, image-guided feature upsampler, which largely mitigates the problem. We also introduce CineMatte-4K, a 4K HDR image-video dataset captured on a professional LED VP stage. To the best of our knowledge, the image subset is the first dataset for VP matting and is non-synthetic, obtained via green-screen insertion; the video subset includes camera motion with tracked trajectories so that arbitrary backgrounds can be rendered later with correct parallax. Across CineMatte-4K and public benchmarks (VideoMatte240K, YouTubeMatte), CineMatte not only excels in VP but also generalizes robustly to real-world footage.

</details>


### [211] [3D Skew Gaussian Splatting with Any Camera Trajectory Visualization Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18334)
*Beizhen Zhao,Yifan Zhou,Gaochao Song,Ziran Yin,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 3DSGS通过将标准高斯原语扩展为偏斜高斯分布，并配合增强的不透明度表示和深度感知稠密化策略，解决了3D高斯泼溅中对称分布导致的视觉伪影问题，实现了更高质量和更紧凑的实时场景表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3DGS依赖对称高斯分布，难以捕捉形状和颜色的不连续性，导致模糊和原语冗余，误导视觉分析中的人类感知。需要一种能处理非对称结构的显式场景表示方法。

Method: 1) 将标准高斯原语扩展为偏斜高斯分布，继承高效光栅化特性同时获得内在非对称建模能力；2) 增强不透明度表示以处理复杂透明度；3) 深度感知稠密化策略优化原语分配；4) 重新推导CUDA光栅化管线以同时支持对称和偏斜高斯，并集成到解耦的自由相机交互式可视化引擎中。

Result: 在细节复杂区域实现了更优的渲染质量和结构紧凑性，同时保持实时帧率，支持流畅的交互式探索。

Conclusion: 3DSGS有效提升了显式场景表示的结构保真度和紧凑性，为实时照片级视图合成中的视觉分析提供了更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has revolutionized real-time photorealistic view synthesis, its fundamental reliance on symmetric Gaussian distributions introduces visual artifacts that hinder accurate spatial data exploration. Specifically, symmetric kernels struggle to capture shape and color discontinuities , which cause blurriness and primitive redundancy that mislead human perception during visual analysis. To address these visualization barriers, we introduce 3D Skew Gaussian Splatting (3DSGS), a novel framework that significantly enhances the structural fidelity and compactness of explicit scene representations. Our key insight lies in extending the standard primitive to a general Skew Gaussian counterpart. This generalized primitive inherits the highly efficient rasterization properties of standard Gaussians while gaining intrinsic asymmetric modeling capabilities. We couple this with an enhanced opacity representation to better handle complex transparency, alongside a depth-aware densification strategy that intelligently manages primitive allocation. Furthermore, to make these advancements actionable for real-world visual analytics, we re-derive the CUDA rasterization pipeline to universally support both symmetric and skew Gaussians, integrating it into a decoupled, free-camera interactive visualization engine. Extensive experiments demonstrate that 3DSGS achieves superior rendering quality and structural compactness, particularly in regions with intricate details, while maintaining the real-time frame rates necessary for fluid interactive exploration. Supplementary derivations and visual results are available at \textbf{\textit{https://3d-skew-gs.github.io/}}.

</details>


### [212] [Focused Forcing: Content-Aware Per-Frame KV Selection for Efficient Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18346)
*Peiliang Cai,Evelyn Zhang,Jiacheng Liu,Hao Lin,Ruiqi Zhang,Weile Mo,Yue Ma,Shikang Zheng,Jiehang Huang,Dongrui Liu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Focused Forcing，一种无需训练的KV缓存选择方法，通过结合注意力分数和多样性分数为每个生成帧选择最相关的历史帧，并根据估计的头部重要性分配预算，在自回归视频扩散模型中实现1.48倍加速，同时提升视觉质量和文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型生成长序列时需要大量KV缓存，现有压缩方法在（1）同一块内多个帧共享相同历史选择，（2）仅用注意力分数评分历史帧，（3）头部预算分配缺乏显式重要性估计等方面存在不足。本文发现同一个生成块内不同帧依赖不同历史帧、同一历史帧的注意力分数随相对时间距离变化、不同注意力头对生成的退化影响不等。

Method: 提出Focused Forcing，一种训练免费的KV选择方法。沿生成帧维度和注意力头维度聚焦缓存历史：为每个生成帧结合注意力分数与多样性分数保留最相关且最具区分度的历史帧，同时为估计重要性更高的头部分配更大预算。

Result: 在多个自回归生成范式下，Focused Forcing实现了最高1.48倍端到端加速（无需训练），同时提升了视觉质量和文本对齐效果。

Conclusion: 通过更细粒度的（帧级和头部级）历史选择策略，在不牺牲质量的前提下有效压缩KV缓存，提升长序列视频生成的效率。代码将开源。

Abstract: Recent advances in autoregressive video diffusion have enabled sequential and streaming video generation. However, long-horizon generation requires increasingly large KV caches, making efficient compression without sacrificing quality challenging. Existing methods mostly select historical frames based on attention scores, but their context decisions remain coarse. When multiple frames are generated in the same chunk, these methods often apply a shared history selection to the whole chunk, score historical frames solely by attention, and assign head-wise budgets either uniformly or by attention-pattern heuristics rather than explicit head-importance estimation. We show that frames within the same generated chunk can depend on distinct historical frames, that the same historical frame can receive different attention scores as its relative temporal distance to the current frames changes, and that masking different heads induces unequal generation degradation. Motivated by these findings, we propose \textbf{Focused Forcing}, a training-free KV selection method that focuses cached history along both generated-frame and head dimensions. For each generated frame, Focused Forcing preserves the most relevant and distinctive historical frames by combining attention scores with diversity scores of historical frames, while assigning larger budgets to heads with higher estimated importance. Across multiple autoregressive generation paradigms, Focused Forcing achieves up to $\textbf{1.48}\times$ end-to-end acceleration without training, while \textbf{improving visual quality and text alignment}. \textit{Our code will be released on GitHub.}

</details>


### [213] [GeoFlow: Enforcing Implicit Geometric Consistency in Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18365)
*Jan Ackermann,Shengqu Cai,Boyang Deng,Zhengfei Kuang,Songyou Peng,Gordon Wetzstein*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出几何一致性奖励，通过强化学习微调将几何一致性作为显式优化目标，显著减少视频生成中的几何伪影，且不依赖特定模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频扩散模型在几何一致性上存在缺陷（目标变形、纹理漂移、非刚性背景），现有解决方案要么是副产品、仅适用于静态场景，要么需要完全重对齐潜在空间，缺乏直接优化几何一致性的方法。

Method: 定义几何一致性奖励函数：利用光流、深度-姿态预测和特征对应，将背景运动与刚性相机诱导流对齐，同时确保独立运动目标沿轨迹保持外观一致性；将该奖励与强化学习微调（RL fine-tuning）结合，使几何一致性成为显式优化目标。

Result: 在包含相机和物体运动的动态场景上，该方法较强大基线显著减少了时序几何伪影，同时保持了感知质量。

Conclusion: 几何一致性奖励结合强化学习微调是一种模型无关的有效策略，能将视频生成中几何一致性从隐性副产品转变为可直接优化的目标，代码和权重已开源。

Abstract: Generating geometrically consistent videos remains an open challenge: text-to-video diffusion models trained on web-scale data treat geometry only implicitly, leading to object deformation, texture drift, and non-rigid backgrounds under camera motion. Existing solutions either improve consistency as a byproduct, apply only to static scenes or realign the latent space of the model completely. We introduce a geometry-consistency reward that directly measures whether motion in a generated video is compatible with a coherent scene. Our key insight is that in physically consistent videos, background motion should be explainable by rigid camera-induced flow, while independently moving objects should preserve appearance identity along motion trajectories. We operationalize this using optical flow, depth--pose predictions, and feature-based correspondence to separate rigid and dynamic regions and evaluate their respective consistency. Integrating this reward with reinforcement fine-tuning transforms geometric consistency from an emergent property into an explicit optimization objective for video generators. The approach is model agnostic and applies to diverse dynamic scenes containing both camera and object motion. Experiments show substantial reductions in temporal geometric artifacts over strong baselines while preserving perceptual quality. Code and model weights are published.

</details>


### [214] [Vision Foundation Models as Generalist Tokenizers for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18390)
*Anlin Zheng,Qi Han,Xin Wen,Chuofan Ma,Lanxi Gong,Gang Yu,Xiangyu Zhang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VFMTok，一种基于冻结视觉基础模型（VFM）的通用图像分词器，通过区域自适应量化框架消除空间冗余，并结合语义重建目标保持语义保真度。在离散和连续潜空间中均取得SOTA生成质量（gFID 1.36和1.25），加速模型收敛3倍，且无需无分类器引导即可实现高保真生成。研究发现VFM预训练中联合优化全局对比学习与潜掩码图像建模能提供最佳分词表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在冻结的视觉基础模型（VFM）之上构建通用图像分词器的可能性，解决现有分词器在空间冗余和语义保真度方面的不足，并揭示VFM预训练目标对分词效果的影响机制。

Method: 提出VFMTok，包含两个关键创新：(1) 区域自适应量化框架，消除标准2D网格特征中的空间冗余；(2) 语义重建目标，使解码输出与VFM表示对齐以保持语义保真度。系统研究了不同VFM预训练目标（全局对比学习、潜掩码图像建模等）对分词效果的影响。

Result: 离散自回归生成中模型收敛加速3倍，ImageNet类别条件合成gFID达1.36（SOTA）；连续空间生成gFID达1.25。无需无分类器引导即可实现高保真类别条件合成，显著加速推理速度。

Conclusion: 冻结VFM可作为通用图像分词器的有效编码器，其中联合优化全局对比学习与潜掩码图像建模的VFM提供最优分词表示。该发现为未来图像分词器设计提供了有价值的指导。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the largely unexplored direction of building a generalist image tokenizer directly on top of a frozen vision foundation model (VFM). To build this tokenizer, we utilize a frozen VFM as the encoder and introduce two key innovations: (1) a region-adaptive quantization framework to eliminate spatial redundancy in standard 2D grid features, and (2) a semantic reconstruction objective that aligns the decoded outputs with the VFM's representations to preserve semantic fidelity. Grounded in these designs, we propose VFMTok, a generalist visual tokenizer capable of operating seamlessly in both discrete and continuous latent spaces. VFMTok achieves substantial improvements in synthesis quality while drastically enhancing token efficiency. For discrete autoregressive (AR) generation, it accelerates model convergence by \textbf{3 times} and achieves a state-of-the-art gFID of \textbf{1.36} on ImageNet class-conditional synthesis. Similarly, for continuous-space generation, integrating VFMTok with a denoising model yields an exceptional gFID of \textbf{1.25}. Furthermore, because the latent space inherently captures rich spatial semantics, VFMTok enables high-fidelity class-conditional synthesis without classifier-free guidance (\textbf{w/o CFG}) across both generative paradigms, significantly accelerating inference speed. Beyond these remarkable empirical results, we systematically investigate the underlying mechanisms of our approach. We discover that the specific self-supervised learning objectives utilized during VFM pre-training dictate its effectiveness as a tokenizer. Specifically, a VFM jointly optimized with global contrastive learning and latent masked image modeling provides the optimal representations for image tokenization. These insights establish a strong foundation and offer valuable guidance for the design of future image tokenizers.

</details>


### [215] [NEWTON: Agentic Planning for Physically Grounded Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18396)
*Yuxiang Feng,Juncheng Wang,Chao Xu,Yijie Qian,Huihan Wang,Wenlong Hou,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Yong Liu,Shujun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视频生成模型在物理常识方面存在系统性缺陷，根本原因是文本提示对物理世界的有损压缩（规范瓶颈）。本文提出NEWTON框架，将视频生成降级为智能体工具箱中的一个动作，通过可学习规划器编排物理感知工具（关键帧生成、科学计算、提示精炼）并配合验证器进行迭代重规划。在VideoPhy-2上，该方法在不修改生成器的情况下将LTX-Video的联合准确率从21.4%提升至29.7%，Veo-3.1从30.7%提升至37.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型在物理常识推理上表现糟糕（VideoPhy-2上最佳模型仅32.6%联合准确率），原因在于文本提示作为物理世界的有损压缩丢失了完整决定动力学的参数，且模型规模扩大无法恢复未指定的信息。

Method: 提出NEWTON框架，将视频生成降级为智能体工具箱中的一个动作。核心组件包括：(1) 可学习规划器，通过Flow-GRPO在多轮交互循环中进行在线策略优化；(2) 物理感知工具集，包括关键帧生成、科学计算、提示精炼等；(3) 验证器，用于闭环迭代重规划。规划器是唯一可训练组件。

Result: 在VideoPhy-2基准上，NEWTON将LTX-Video的联合准确率从21.4%提升至29.7%，将Veo-3.1从30.7%提升至37.4%，且无需修改原始生成器。

Conclusion: 物理条件化需要同时满足充分性、动态性和可验证性三个属性，现有方法均无法同时满足。NEWTON通过智能体工具箱范式和在线策略优化有效弥合了规范瓶颈，为物理常识感知的视频生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Video generation models produce visually compelling results but systematically violate physical commonsense -- on VideoPhy-2, the best model achieves only 32.6% joint accuracy. We identify a specification bottleneck: text prompts are lossy compression of the physical world, omitting the parameters that fully determine dynamics, and no amount of model scaling can recover what was never specified. From this diagnosis we derive three properties that physics conditioning must satisfy -- sufficiency, dynamism, and verifiability -- and show that no existing approach satisfies all three.
